The action queue carries `people:identity` (nav `labor/people`).
- **Roles held.** `POST people/<key>/roles` `{role, since?, primary?, remove?}` (TEAM_RATE) — "trained on bar from 9/1" (a candidate for a bartender gap from then) or, with `primary`, a promotion (their role on the roster) → `{ok, name, role, since (ISO), since_label (M/D/YY), primary}`; `{remove: true, role}` → `{ok, removed: true}` (404 when they don't hold it); 404 for a name not on the roster.
- **Guest mentions.** `GET people/mentions` (LABOR_VIEW) → `{ok, mentions: [{id, name, key, date (M/D/YY), date_iso, polarity, review_id, snippet, status}], can_confirm}` — guests naming someone on staff, waiting on confirmation before they count on the person's record. `POST people/mentions/<id>` `{confirm: bool}` (TEAM_RATE) → `{ok, confirmed, told}`; 409 once answered. A confirmed positive mention also tells the employee ("A guest named you", `staff_insights.tell_recognition` through `people.tell`); `told` is `push|sms|email|null`, and the confirm never fails on it.
- **The person record** (`GET people/<key>`) adds `roles_held: [{role, since, primary}]`, `covers: {taken, declined, days: 180}` (null when unreadable), `guest_mentions` (up to 5 confirmed) and `attendance` — `{known: true, shifts, missed, late, no_show_rate, unreliable, last_miss}`, or `{known: false}` when nobody watched their shifts: say "Not watched yet", never a clean record.
- **Covers.** `POST issues/<id>/cover-answer` `{name, accepted: bool}` (LABOR_VIEW) — the manager's word on "Did Zed take it?" for a coverage issue → `{ok, answer: took | declined}`; 400 without both, 404 for an issue that is not a coverage issue of this restaurant. It stands over what the nightly job infers from the punches (`people.answer_cover`) and is kept on the issue's own `meta.asked[].answer` / `answered_at` so both Homes stop asking (`intraday.mark_cover_answer`); a different answer once one is kept is a 409 `{ok: false, answer, error}` — a stale screen's tap never overwrites it (re-audit 9/29/26, INVENTORY-10). Replacement suggestions (`labor_replacements.for_gap` — the pre-dinner pulse, the close-out and schedule learning) carry `covers_taken` / `covers_declined` and rank by them after the owner's rating.
- **Capability history.** `GET /api/labor/capability-changes` and `/mobile/api/labor/capability-changes` (one body, `mobile_api.mobile_capability_changes`) → `{ok, changes: [{id, restaurant_id, kind (rating | threshold | leader_rule | profile | weights), subject, before, after, changed_by, changed_at}]}`, newest 100 — who changed a rating, a target, a profile or the weighting, and when. The Team sheet reads it.
- **Learned patterns.** `GET labor/learned-patterns` adds `standing: [{key, kind, employee, role, day, daypart, time, text, editors, first_learned (M/D/YY), first_learned_iso, last_confirmed, last_confirmed_iso, times_applied, times_overridden, status (active | retired | ruled), can_be_rule, rule: {note, by} | null}]` — what the draft keeps after the manager stopped correcting it — `conflicts: [{employee, day, daypart, off_by, on_by, text}]` (two editors pulling one person, day and daypart opposite ways: left out of the draft for the owner to settle, never the model to guess) and `can_edit`. `POST labor/learned-patterns` `{key, rule: true}` is "Make it a rule" (SCHEDULE_DRAFT): a person pattern is written into that person's own settings — taken off a daypart becomes that daypart unavailable on that day, put on becomes a preferred daypart — the standing row turns `ruled`, and it answers `{ok, key, employee, rule, kind}`; 400 for a key the draft has not learned, a pattern that is not about one person, or one that would leave them no day to work. Schedule audit 10/3/26: each live pattern carries `opportunities`, `rate` and `confidence` (L-6 — the weeks it could have been made in, the recency-weighted share it was, its Wilson bound; `active` needs two weeks and half of them) and `dismissed_by_admin` (an admin's view-as dismissal, not counted until adopted — L-10); `standing` rows add `opportunities`, `hits`, `confidence`, `last_hand` / `last_hand_iso`, `retest_since` (M/D/YY while the next draft leaves it out to check it is still wanted — L-30), `retired_reason` (reversed | retest | decayed | owner), `source` (learned | owner_said) and status `retest`; the response adds `admin_saves` `{versions, weeks, answers}`, `admin_dismissals` and `can_adopt`. "Make it a rule" (L-33) also turns a `headcount_add` into a role floor (a note rule at the number the manager settled on there) and a `retime_start` / `retime_end` into a role start/end rule the draft is retimed to and checked against — both the account owner's only (403 otherwise); a `headcount_cut` is a 400 ("Cavnar AI holds staffing minimums, not maximums"). `{key, keep: true}` is a hand confirmation (any re-test ended), `{key, keep: false}` lets it go (retired, `owner`); 400 through view-as. `POST labor/learned-patterns/adopt` and `POST labor/schedule/adopt-admin-saves` `{history_id?}` (both prefixes; the account holder signed in as themselves, else 403) count an admin's pattern dismissals, or schedule saves and one-tap answers, as theirs → `{ok, adopted}` / `{ok, versions, answers}` (L-8, L-10), each recorded in the account log.
- **Schedule result** (the generation payload): `projected_revenue_source` now also reads "your budget (6 of 7 nights budgeted)" when the owner budgeted at least 5 of the week's nights in the DSR (the rest filled from their usual nights), or "the week's day-by-day projection …" (`schedule_economics.projected_weekly_revenue`: the budget, else `demand.week_projection` corrected by its own `revenue_week` record, else the median week; the object adds `raw_value`, `calibration` and `estimator`); `labor_target_label` / `labor_target_source` (the target it judged against, "your goal …" while a goal holds); `soft_requirements: [{source: reviews | dsr, day, date, daypart, role, text, confirm, expires, key, scheduled, typical, applied}]` — the reviews' and the report's staffing asks for the week and whether the draft put more of that role on the slot than typical (read from the rows, not the model's word; also in `review.soft_requirements`); `pattern_conflicts` (as `conflicts` above).
- **The scheduling memory** (schedule audit 10/3/26 L-29, H2). `GET labor/schedule-memory` (`?retired=0` leaves the retired out; LABOR_VIEW) — `{ok, items: [{key, kind, fact_class, class_label, person, role, day, daypart, text, value, status (candidate | active | rule | retest | retired | dormant), status_label (Learning | Applied | A rule | Being re-tested | Retired | Asleep), enforcement (prompt | soft | hard), held_in_code, bound_by, confidence (0-1), confidence_pct ("72%", "—" below two opportunities), opportunities, hits, misses_by_hand, last_confirmed_by_hand / first_seen / last_seen (M/D/YY), source, origin, retired_reason, retired_words, owner_said, rule, can_keep, can_let_go, can_be_rule}], counts: {status: n}, consolidated_at (M/D/YY), classes, ladder: {candidate_min_weeks, candidate_min_rate, active_confidence, retire_confidence}, can_answer, can_make_rules}`. `POST labor/schedule-memory` `{key, action: keep | let_go | rule}` (SCHEDULE_DRAFT; a rule other than a person's own slot pattern is the account owner's — 403) → `{ok, key, action, rule}`: keep applies it while its evidence holds, let_go retires it (only newer hand evidence brings it back), rule makes it a rule the draft keeps — a pattern through `schedule_versions.make_rule`, a pair → the owner's pairing, an opener → their standing shift, a closing overrun → the role's end-time rule; 400 with the owner's words for one that cannot (overtime, a section, a redo reason, another learner's fact) and through view-as. Both prefixes (`/api/…`, `/mobile/api/…`). Re-audit 10/4/26 (LEARN-3, LEARN-6): each item also carries `answered` ("Kept by you" / "Kept by the owner" / "Let go by a manager") and `keep_label` ("Keep them apart" for a learned keep-apart, else "Keep"); a login that is not an account holder never receives a learned keep-apart, and its `can_keep` / `can_let_go` are false where the owner already answered the other way. The POST answers `{…, message}` (what the answer did, in the answerer's words) or, on a refusal, 400: a manager undoing the owner's keep or let-go, answering a keep-apart, or answering a fact that is a rule. `POST labor/learned-patterns` refuses (400, in words) a manager letting go, dismissing or restoring against the owner's answer, and an admin restoring the owner's dismissal.
- **Measured ratings** (L-23, D-27). `GET labor/ratings/suggested` — the account holder only (403 otherwise): `{ok, available, window: [iso, iso], servers: [{name, tickets, covers, sales_per_cover, house_sales_per_cover, ratio, suggested (1-5), current (their counted rating, null = not rated), differs, reason}], min_tickets, note}` — what each server on the roster sells a guest against the house at the same mealtimes over 56 days, past 20 tickets with a guest count. `POST labor/ratings/suggested` `{name, score?}` — the account holder confirms the suggestion (or their own 1-5) → it is their Operational Score (`authority` principal) → `{ok, name, score, suggested}`; 403 for anyone else, 400 through view-as or for a name with no suggestion. The action queue carries `ratings:measured` ("N servers measured by the POS — confirm their ratings", nav `labor/ratings`) for the account holder, and `calibration:weights` ("Shift Quality calibration has a strong suggestion…", nav `labor/intel`) while a strong weekly suggestion is unapplied (L-14).
- **What the published weeks did** (L-12, L-13). `GET labor/intel` `outcomes[weekday][daypart]` adds `avg_people`, `actual_weeks`, `avg_actual_hours` / `avg_actual_people` (the punches; null with none), `missed`, `stayed_late`, `labor_pct` (the daypart's own, null unless measured) and `splh_basis` (`worked` | `scheduled`: sales per labor hour is over the hours worked where the weeks have them).
- **Schedule suppression.** `GET labor/intel` adds `recommendation_suppression: {kind: {state: suppressed | retest, reason, since, review_on (M/D/YY), retests}}` beside `suppressed_recommendation_kinds`, and `POST labor/schedule/recommendation` answers `suppression` in the same shape — why each schedule kind is off and when it is re-tested. Only the owner's showings and answers suppress a kind.
- **Over target, fitted.** The web Labor page context and `/mobile/api/labor` carry `over_margin` (the points over target a day must run to count as over — this restaurant's own daily swing, never below `thresholds.LABOR_OVER_TARGET_PTS`, 3) and `over_margin_basis` (words), live data only (null on sample shifts).
- **Trend and export.** `GET /api/labor-trend` and `/mobile/api/labor/trend` `weeks[]` add `complete` (a payroll week still in progress is drawn as partial, never read as a trend), `comparable` and `basis` ("recosted, not comparable" when the cost source changed). The `labor.csv` export's columns are `week_start, week_end, labor_pct, labor_cost, sales, days_with_sales, complete`.
- **Events, measured.** `GET labor/demand-signals` gives each `event` signal `measured: {median_lift_pct, n, applies, last, text} | null` (its label's own record here — before and after, not proof) and adds `what_nights_teach: [{label, display, kind, n, median_lift_pct, low_lift_pct, high_lift_pct, direction, last_date, applies, owner_median_pct, text}]` (`event_memory.summaries`, the "What your nights have taught" card), and `follows: [{series_id, slug, name, category, following, source, distance_km, in_reach, next}]` — the calendars followed for this location or within reach (Event Intelligence, 10/1/26; `[]` when the catalog can't be read).
- **Follow a calendar.** `POST labor/event-follows/<series_id>` `{active: true|false}` (web `/api/…`, mobile `/mobile/api/…`; the schedule editors' — `SCHEDULE_DRAFT`) → `{ok, follows, refreshing, message}` (event re-audit 2, 10/1/26 — `added`/`removed` are gone): the owner's choice stands over auto-follow and is committed in the request; the restaurant's copy of the games is re-synced just after, on ops' bounded admin task pool (`ops.run_admin_task`, an `event_sync_one` job run; a second press joins the pending one), never inside the request. `refreshing` says it was queued, and `message` says when the games arrive or leave ("in a moment", or "with the 5am event sync" when the pool could not take it — the change still stands and the answer is still 200). 400 without a boolean `active`, 403 for a login that can't edit the schedule's inputs, 404 for a series not in the catalog (nothing queued). Recorded in Account activity (`event_follow_set`, "Game calendar changed", the detail naming the calendar); the follow row's `source` is `admin` when an admin or support login made the change through view-as, else `owner`. Each followed row also carries `season: {text, measured, played, incremental, basis} | null` (phase 3: the season's measured game nights over their usual weekday — what the games brought, not Cavnar AI's value).
- **Games removed, and Put back.** `GET labor/event-dismissals` (labor viewers) → `{ok, removed: [{event_id, series_id, name, text, date, removed_on, removed_by, by_admin}]}` — the catalog games this restaurant removed (✕ on a calendar row on the web, a swipe on iOS), newest first, `text` the game on the restaurant's clock, `removed_on` M/D/YY on that clock, `removed_by` the login's name (an admin's through view-as, with `by_admin`). `POST labor/event-dismissals/<event_id>/restore` (the schedule editors' — `SCHEDULE_DRAFT`) → `{ok, removed, refreshing, message}`: the removal is lifted in the request and the restaurant's copy of the games re-synced just after on the same admin task pool (`event_sync_one`), `refreshing` and `message` as for a follow; 404 when that game isn't removed here (a retry after a lifted removal gets it too; another restaurant's game is a 404), 403 for a login that can't edit the schedule's inputs. Recorded in Account activity (`event_game_restored`, "Removed game put back", the game on the restaurant's clock). Put back is the one way back: a re-follow leaves removals alone.
- **Game this week.** `GET food-cost/game-week` (web and mobile; the Food view's) → `{ok, game: {event_id, describe, text, order: {lines: [{ingredient, unit, extra, items}], n, text, basis} | null, items, others, basis} | null}` — the next followed game within 7 days: the extra each ingredient games like it used here, through the recipes, once two clean ones are measured; else what the last one sold, as one game, and why there is no plan (one game, nights with something else on, not every game). With two headline games on that date the one with a measured effect leads and `others` holds the rest, named in `text` ("Also that day: …"). Nothing is added to an order.

### Reviews (`/mobile/api/reviews/*`, `/mobile/api/review-stats`; web routes are flat — `/api/reviews/page`, `/api/review-stats`, `/api/topic-heatmap`, `/api/sentiment-trend`, `/api/response-performance`, `/api/regenerate-draft/<id>`, `/api/save-draft/<id>`, and the actions at root: `/approve/<id>`, `/skip/<id>`, `/undo/<id>`, `/retract/<id>`)
List/detail/approve/skip/regenerate a draft response (skip — `/skip/<id>`, `/mobile/api/reviews/<id>/skip`, Ask's `skip_review`, one body `client_api._do_skip` — refuses an approved or posted reply with `409 {ok: false, response_status, error}` naming undo / retract; it used to mark a live reply skipped), retry a failed Google post (`/reviews/<id>/retry-post`, both surfaces), bulk approve-all, sentiment trend, topic heatmap, response-performance, review-request send, review stats. Approve-all (NS5 M10) runs `ai_guard.check_review_reply` on each reply first; one that fails is marked `draft_needs_review` with the reason and not posted — the reply adds `held_for_review` (how many this run held), and Home's cache is dropped when anything was approved or held. The stored `draft_review_reason` is the plain verb phrase every surface reads after "This reply …" (no "held from …:" prefix; `drafter.owner_reason` cleans an older row); the approve refusal reads `Read this reply before you post it: it <reason>. Open the review to post it anyway.` Approve-all also takes `review_ids` (web and mobile): the replies a confirm card listed (`ask_cavnar_tools.bulk_publish_preview`, the same set and checks — a reply the check would reword is held, not listed) — only those post, and only while still publishable; `[]` posts nothing; without it, every publishable draft up to `limit` (re-audit F1-9). The `approve_all_reviews` proposal's `body` carries them.

**Answered outside Cavnar AI** (10/2/26): `POST /api/reviews/<id>/replied-elsewhere` and `POST /mobile/api/reviews/<id>/replied-elsewhere` (one body, `client_api._do_replied_elsewhere`, any console login) mark a review someone already answered on Google by hand — posted, `response_action` replied_elsewhere, out of the queue, the urgent count and the reminders; nothing is posted. 200 (`already` when it was), 404, 409 for a reply approved in Cavnar AI. `/undo/<id>` and `/mobile/api/reviews/<id>/undo` take the mark off; `/retract/<id>` refuses it (409) — never deletes a reply someone else wrote. Rows carry `replied_elsewhere` and `external_reply`; `can_retract` is false for them.
`GET /api/reviews/page` (web only — the inbox's server-rendered page; iOS reads `/mobile/api/reviews`): `offset`, `filter` (all | urgent | pending | positive | neutral | negative), `search` (author or text), `category`, `platform` → `{ok, html, count, total, offset, has_more}`. The web inbox asks it whenever a filter or search runs over a partial page (friction #20) and replaces the list, so "No reviews match" is never said about a review on page 2. `review_id=<id>` (friction #1) returns that one card whatever the other params say, scoped to the login's restaurant (another location's id is `count: 0`; a non-integer is 400) — what `rvFocusReview` / the `review/<id>` nav path fetch when the card is not loaded.

**Approving a flagged draft** (parity round, 9/25/26): `POST /approve/<id>` and `POST /mobile/api/reviews/<id>/approve` take an optional JSON body `{confirm_flagged: true}`. A drafted reply the reply guard flagged (`draft_needs_review`) is refused without it — `409 {ok: false, needs_review: true, review_reason, error}` — and nothing is posted; the flag is held inside the approve's compare-and-set (`models.claim_approval(allow_flagged=False)`), so a draft flagged after the screen loaded is refused too. Web and iOS send `confirm_flagged: true` only after showing the reason and the owner choosing "Post it anyway"; the lock-screen action, the iOS swipe, the notification row, Ask's `approve_review` proposal, the auto-approve rule and a queued offline replay of an unconfirmed approve send nothing and get the 409. `POST /api/regenerate-draft/<id>`, `/api/save-draft/<id>` and their mobile twins already answer `needs_review` / `review_reason`; both clients now update the flag from them.

**Approving the reply you read** (iOS audit, 9/25/26): `POST /mobile/api/reviews/<id>/approve` also takes an optional `expected_draft` — the reply text on the owner's screen when they tapped Approve. It is held inside the approve's compare-and-set (`models.claim_approval(expected_draft=...)`, compared as sent and stripped), so an approve that lands late — the phone's offline queue — never posts a different reply than the one approved: `409 {ok: false, draft_changed: true, error}` and nothing is posted. The phone sends it on every approve, live or queued. `GET /mobile/api/reviews` and `/mobile/api/reviews/<id>` send `draft_needs_review` and `draft_edited` as JSON booleans (they were raw SQLite 0/1, which the phone's decoder refused).

`GET /mobile/api/reviews` takes `search` (author or text, `models.get_reviews_data`, the same match as the web's `/api/reviews/page`) with `limit`/`offset`; iOS sends it debounced (350ms) and keeps it on every following page.

**Row fields both inboxes read** (parity audit 10/7/26): every row carries `post_failed` (`models.review_post_failed`: approved, Google, Business Profile connected, never posted — the web card's "Couldn't post to Google" + Retry and the phone's pill read this one field), `urgent` (`models.is_urgent_review`; the phone no longer recomputes it), `severity_reason`, and — always present, `null` when unset — `external_reply`, `external_reply_source` (`google` | `owner`), `external_reply_at` and `posted_at`, so an answered-elsewhere review shows the reply actually posted. `GET /api/review-stats` and `/mobile/api/review-stats` add `publishable` (what one bulk publish may post, `models.reply_queue_counts`) and `publish_held` — the phone's "Publish N ready". A person's approve (`/approve/<id>`, `/mobile/api/reviews/<id>/approve`) now takes a **skipped** reply too ("Approve after all"; it was refused as "no drafted reply"); the auto-approve rule and a bulk publish never do.

Inbox order (density round 9/25/26, #33): `get_reviews_data` sorts every review whose reply is posted, approved or skipped below every one still waiting, then by urgency, severity, sentiment and date — on both surfaces. The web draws an "Answered (N)" divider where they begin.

`GET /api/reviews/why-line` (web) and `GET /mobile/api/reviews/why-line` (phone twin), both over `review_intelligence.inbox_why` → `{ok, rating_delta, recent_n, prior_n, window_days, complaint}`. `rating_delta` is the average star rating of the last 28 days against the 28 before, `null` (never 0) unless each side holds 3 reviews; `complaint` is the top stored diagnosis — `{category, label, mentions, window_days, stale, as_of}` — or `null`. Counts and stored rows only, no model call. Gated to Reviews by the `/api/reviews` prefix.

**Memory round (9/29/26) — Reviews.**
- `POST /api/reviews/<id>/retag` and `/mobile/api/reviews/<id>/retag` (a `strategy_routes._ROUTES` entry, `review_signals.retag`) `{categories?, sentiment?, severity?, dishes?}` in the analyser's own vocabulary → `{ok, changed: [fields], review: {id, categories, sentiment, severity, dishes}}` ("Fix tags" on a review card); 400 when nothing is sent or a value is outside the vocabulary (the reason names it), 404 for a review that is not this restaurant's. Each changed field is kept in `review_retags` with who made it and their authority, and the analyser learns this restaurant's tags from the recent ones (never an admin's or a view-as correction).
- `GET /api/review-request-stats` and `/mobile/api/review-request-stats` add `conversion: {asked, reviewed, pct, window_days (14), basis}` (`review_signals.request_conversion`): requests whose 14 days have passed and how many produced a review, matched by the guest's name only when exactly one fits; `pct` is null under 10 such requests — show "—".
- Team access (`POST /api/account/team/<id>/access`, `permissions.GRANTABLE`) offers `reviews.voice`, "Writes replies in our voice": a manager or member with it has their approved replies and edits teach the drafter as the owner's do. It opens no route and never earns auto-approve trust. Since the memory re-audit fix round it also offers `marketing.voice`, "Writes posts in our voice": that login's sent posts, emails and texts teach the marketing voice block the same way.
- Who approved is recorded with every approval: `client_api._do_approve(…, user=)` and `_do_approve_all(…, user=)` take the approving login from every approve route, web and mobile, single and bulk (Ask's confirmed card posts the same routes); the scheduler's auto-approve records itself as automatic (`models.reply_approver`: the login, its `answer_authority`, normal or view-as). Support approving through view-as is logged `support_approved` and left out of trust, the drafter's examples and edit learning.

### Food Cost (`/mobile/api/food-cost/*`, `/api/food-cost*`)
Inventory CSV upload, custom items, purchase orders, waste/drift insight.

Friction audit (9/25/26, workstream O2) — every route below at `/api/…` and `/mobile/api/…`, one body each:
- `POST food-cost/send-order` also takes `lines: [{ingredient_id | item, qty}]` with a single `supplier_email` — the owner's quantities on that supplier's order (0 takes a line off; only lines on the draft; 400 for a bad number, for edits across several suppliers, or when every line is 0). Checked against `draft_hash` first; the PO records the draft beside the sent lines (`edited`). A queued send carries the edits in its `delayed` payload.
- `GET food-cost/purchase-orders` — without FOOD_COST_VIEW (a manager receiving a delivery) every `total_cost` / `unit_cost` / `line_cost` is taken off (`client_api.purchase_orders_for`).
- `POST food-cost/purchase-orders/<id>/received` `{lines?: [{ingredient_id | item, qty}]}` — closes the PO **and posts each line with an ingredient into stock** (`inventory_ledger.record_receiving`, source `purchase_order`) at the ordered quantity or the one given for a short line. `{ok, po_number, posted: [{item, qty, unit}], short: [{item, ordered, received}], skipped: [names with no ingredient]}`; 404 when already received or not this restaurant's (the status flip claims the PO first, so nothing posts twice). A failure partway reopens the PO and answers `503 {retry: true, posted}`; a retry posts only the lines not already in stock (each receiving event's note starts with the PO number; F2-18).
- `POST food-cost/ingredients` `{name, unit?, unit_cost?}` — a new ingredient (an unmatched invoice line); an active ingredient of the same name is returned with `existed: true`. 400 on no name or a bad cost.
- `POST food-cost/waste` `{ingredient_id, qty, reason?}` — one line of logged waste (`inventory_ledger.record_waste`, source `logged`, so `waste_sources` counts it as counted, not inferred). `reason` ∈ spoiled, dropped, overprepped, returned, expired, other. 400 on qty ≤ 0 or another restaurant's ingredient.
- `GET food-cost/invoices` rows gain `pending` (never applied, or applied by the trusted-supplier rule with lines left for a person), `waiting` (lines still to settle) and `lines`. `POST food-cost/invoices/<id>/apply` also takes `{use_checked: true}` — apply the stored invoice's preselected lines that are also `verified` (Ask's `apply_invoice_lines`, confirmed without the photo; F2-4); 409 when none are left.
- **Counts without the margins** (`permissions.FOOD_COST_ENTER`, held by every console role incl. `manager`): `GET/POST food-cost/count-sheet`, `GET food-cost/purchase-orders`, `POST food-cost/purchase-orders/<id>/received` and `POST food-cost/waste` pass the inventory module gate on it (`permissions.FOOD_COST_ENTER_PATHS`); every other Food Cost route still needs FOOD_COST_VIEW. `GET food-cost/count-sheet` also returns `source` (`inventory_sync.status`: `{provider, label, connected, synced, synced_at, error}`); once `synced`, the web sheet is read-only and Save count is gone (9/26/26). `GET food-cost/recipes` (web + phone, `FOOD_COST_ENTER`): `{ok, recipes: [{id, name, sell_price, lines: [{name, qty, unit}]}], count, source}` - every dish with a recipe; the web lists them only once `source.synced`. `POST /api/food-cost/waste` now also returns `waste: {week, label, items: [{name, cost, pct, qty}]}`, the week as it stands after the line, so the page updates in place.

Web/iOS parity (9/25/26), Food Cost, exports and module gating:
- **Module tiles carry `mode`.** `/mobile/api/home` and `/mobile/api/home/modules` `modules[]` add `mode: "full" | "counts"` (`mobile_api._module_tile_mode`): a module whose view permission (`permissions.MODULE_VIEW_PERMISSIONS`) this login lacks is left out, and Food Cost for a login with FOOD_COST_ENTER but not FOOD_COST_VIEW is `mode: "counts"` with `kpi {value: "Counts", sublabel: "stock, deliveries, waste"}`, `pulse: null`, no analysis run, and `inventory` left out of Home's `active_keys` (so nothing on Home reads the margins for it). iOS opens `FoodCostCountsOnlyView` for it — the web's Counts tab. A refused route still answers `403 {module_forbidden: true, module}`; iOS reads it as `APIError.Kind.moduleForbidden`.
- `GET food-cost/tracker` (web and mobile, one body `client_api._do_food_cost_tracker`) → `{ok, items: [{name, unit, price, usage, custom}], from_pantry, submitted_at, typed_at, previous_submitted_at, custom_items: [{name, unit}]}` — the rows the price monitor opens on, from `client_api.food_cost_tracker_data` (the same function the web tracker renders at page load): this week's saved prices; for a ledger account the live pantry (`from_pantry: true`, read-only until Edit prices); otherwise the seven defaults plus the saved custom items with blank prices. Prices and usage are strings ("" when blank, never 0).
- `/mobile/api/food-cost/trend` `target` is now drawn from the live analysis exactly as the web card's (`client_api.waste_trend_analysis`, shared): `basis` is `live` whenever there is a live count, `history` only without one — the phone passed no analysis and always drew `history`.
- **iOS parity round (10/7/26), Food Cost.** One waste-trend body, `client_api._do_waste_trend`, behind `GET /api/food-cost/waste-trend`, the new `GET /mobile/api/food-cost/waste-trend?range=8w|13w|26w|all` (the card whole: `range`, `ranges` — only those the history fills — `weeks_total`, `target {pct, weekly, basis, label}`, `weeks` (`week_end`, `start`, `label`, `waste`, `flags`), `stats`, `observations`, `categories`, `empty`) and the older `/mobile/api/food-cost/trend` (kept, same 8-week shape, now read from that body). `POST food-cost/count-sheet` answers **409** `{code: "inventory_synced", source, error}` once an inventory system has synced (`inventory_sync.status`): its counts are the ledger's, on every client. `GET food-cost/tracker` adds `source` (the same status). `POST food-cost/waste` and `POST food-cost/purchase-orders/<id>/received` (web and phone) run through `strategy_routes._idempotent`: an `Idempotency-Key` header or an `idempotency_key` in the JSON body answers a repeat from the first send's result (a receive replay no longer reads "already received"); a repeat still in flight is **409** `{in_progress: true}`, which the phone's offline queue waits on rather than drops.
- `/mobile/api/food-cost/analytics` isolates the AI read: a model failure (a budget stop, an outage) now returns `200 ok: true` with every figure and list, `insight` / `insight_intro` carrying the message and `insight_error` set (null on success). It used to return `ok: false` with every list empty.
- `POST account/export-data` (web and mobile, one body) refuses any scope outside `mobile_api.export_scopes_for(restaurant, user)` — `reviews` and `settings` always, `labor` only with the Labor module and LABOR_VIEW, `food_cost` only with the Food Cost module and FOOD_COST_VIEW. The rest still export and the response names what was dropped in `refused`; `403 {refused}` when nothing is left. `/mobile/api/account` `data` adds `export_scopes` (the list the picker shows).
- `GET account-settings/hours/google` (mobile: `GET account/hours/google`) — Google Business's listed hours in Account's shape `{ok, connected, open: {Day: "HH:MM"}, close: {…}}`; nothing is saved. `connected: false` when Google isn't linked; 502 when Google doesn't answer.
- `POST account-settings/hours` (mobile `account/hours`) is the owner's (`permissions.is_principal`, 403 otherwise — F2-3); `GET /api/account-settings` `hours.can_edit` and the mobile profile's `hours_can_edit` say so. `closures` now writes the **scheduler's closed dates** (`schedule_rules.save_closures`, the list Labor → Scheduling rules edits) — ISO or M/D/YY entries; the rest come back in `ignored`. Only touched when `closures` is sent. It no longer writes `skip_holidays` (the marketing holiday list). `GET /api/account-settings` `hours.closures` and the mobile profile's new `closures` read the same list. A client that sends its whole list also sends `closures_base` (the list it opened with) and only its own adds and removes are applied — the iPhone sheet does; the web no longer sends `closures` here at all.
- `POST account-settings/closures` (mobile `account/closures`) — one closed-date change at a time, `{add: date | [dates]}` or `{remove: …}` (ISO or M/D/YY), applied against what is stored (`schedule_rules.change_closed_dates`), owner-only like hours. Returns `{ok, closures, added, removed}`; 400 names a value that isn't a date. Account and Labor → Scheduling rules both save each chip through it the moment it is added or removed (owner, 9/28/26: a whole list sent with Save hours / Save rules lost dates).
- `GET dsr/budget/prefill?start=YYYY-MM-DD&from=last_week|last_year|forecast&pct=&days=` — owner view only. Suggested figures for the budget editor, nothing saved: `{days: [{date, gross, net, from}], missing: [dates], basis}`. `last_week` = that night's budget a week back, else what it took; `last_year` = the same weekday 52 weeks back (report or imported workbook) × (1 + pct/100), pct −50…100; `forecast` = Cavnar's sales forecast as net (gross left blank). A night with nothing to go on stays blank, never 0. A filled night carries `clear: [fields this source has no figure for]` — the client empties them, so two sources never mix on one night (F2-19).
- `GET labor/covers` gains `pos_offers: [{date, guests}]` — nights the POS counted guests (DSR `sales.guests`) with no cover count; offered only. `POST labor/covers` `{from_pos: true, rows}` stores them with source `pos_confirmed`.
- `GET /api/alert-settings` gains `owner: {name, phone}` — what contact 1 starts as when none is saved.
- **Owner-only account switches (security audit, 9/25/26).** `POST /api/alert-settings` and `POST /mobile/api/account/alert-settings` (every field is a restaurant column — contacts, SMS, which alerts, quiet hours, the digest, `alert_extra_emails`, which `notify.alert_recipients` adds to every alert and digest email; there is no per-login delivery setting to leave a manager), `POST /api/update-digest-day`, and the shared bodies `client_api._do_login_notify` / `_do_monthly_review_pref` / `_do_marketing_opt_out` (web `/api/account-settings/login-notify|monthly-review|marketing-opt-out`, mobile `/mobile/api/account/login-notify|monthly-review|marketing-opt-out`, and Ask's `change_setting`, which now passes the asking login) answer a non-owner `403 {ok: false, owner_only: true, error}` and change nothing (`permissions.principal_only` / `is_principal`).
- **Connections are owner-only.** Google Business (`GET /auth/google/connect`, `GET /auth/google/callback`, `POST /auth/google/disconnect`; mobile `GET /mobile/api/connections/google/authorize`, `DELETE /mobile/api/connections/google`) and Instagram/Facebook (`GET /instagram/connect`, `POST /api/instagram-disconnect`; mobile `GET /mobile/api/connections/instagram/authorize`, `DELETE /mobile/api/connections/instagram`) refuse a non-owner with `403 {owner_only: true}` (the web callback answers the popup with an error page, 403), like Toast, Square, Clover, RPower and webhooks. The session-less callbacks (`/auth/google/mobile-callback`, `/instagram/callback`) trust only the signed state the owner-only start minted.
- **Remembered devices are per login.** `auth.trusted_device_ok(restaurant_id, token, user_id, principal=)` matches the login that ticked "remember" (`trusted_devices.user_id`); every sign-in path (web password, Google SSO, mobile password, Sign in with Apple) calls `auth.remembered_device_ok(user, token)`. A token naming no login (an old row, the legacy `restaurants.two_fa_device_token`) is honoured only for an account holder.
- `GET food-cost/cfo`: each driver may carry `act: {label, nav}`; `GET cross-module` links may carry `act: {label, nav}` and `day` / `dish` — where the finding is acted on, as a nav path (U4-9).
- Web nav heads registered by Food Cost: `invoice/<id>` (opens that scan), `order/<supplier email>` (the order card on that supplier); sections `inventory/invoices`, `inventory/order`, `inventory/count`, `inventory/menu`, `inventory/margins`, `inventory/waste`.

**Memory round (9/29/26) — Food Cost.**
- **Par suggestions** (`strategy_routes._ROUTES`, both prefixes). `GET food-cost/par-suggestions` → `{ok, suggestions: [{ingredient_id, name, unit, par, suggested_par, times, last, key, basis, rec_id}]}` (`ordering.par_suggestions`): items the close-out 86'd (counted to 0 at close) on 2 or more nights in the last 28 days, counting only nights after the par was last set; the suggestion is the larger of a quarter more and a day's usage more, rounded up. Each is keyed `raise_par:<item>`, presented on `food`, and one already answered is left out — "Not now" answers it through `POST recs/event` with the key. `[]` without FOOD_COST_VIEW. `POST food-cost/par-suggestions/<ingredient_id>/accept` `{par?}` (default the suggested par; FOOD_COST_VIEW) → `{ok, ingredient_id, par, was}`: writes the par, records the change in `change_log` and marks the recommendation implemented. 409 when the item has no live suggestion (a par is otherwise the owner's to set from the count sheet); 400 when `par` is not a number above 0.
- **Orders learn the owner's hand.** `GET food-cost/order-draft` (and everything built on `inventory.build_supplier_orders` — send-order, auto-order, Ask) lines may carry `owner_adjusted: {factor, orders, basis}` and `base_qty` (the formula's quantity; `qty` is the corrected one): an ingredient whose draft the owner kept changing across at least 3 of their own sent orders ("Adjusted to how you order: …"). An order sent through an admin's view-as never teaches it.
- **Invoices.** Each scanned line (`food-cost/invoices`) carries `matched_by`: `your_match` (the ingredient the owner picked for this line's words before — "Matched as you did last time"), `name` (matched by name) or null.
- **Reprice.** `GET food-cost/reprice` suggestions add `owner_ratio` (`menu_intelligence.reprice_acceptance`: `{ratio, decisions, median, basis}` once 3 decisions exist, else null), `typical_price` and `typical_monthly` (this owner's usual share of the suggested rise and the monthly margin it recovers — "You usually raise about half — $22.00 would recover $150/mo"), `value_complaints` / `value_note` (guests calling the dish poor value in the recent review window; Home's reprice card on that dish has its confidence capped at 49% with that reason, `home_brief.REPRICE_VALUE_CAP`), and `guard: {kind: "guest_complaints", text, link_key, since}` when guests name the dish in complaints while Food Cost ranks it a driver — show `guard.text` and demote the one-tap button; Home and the action queue do not offer it one-tap.
- **An older diagnosis is not a live recommendation.** `GET food-cost/cfo`'s `diagnosis`, and the Reviews read's `diagnoses[]`, carry `controls_withheld: "stale"` with `answerable: false` when the diagnosis is too old to lean on (or was served on a stale read): hide Done / Pass and label it an older read. It is not presented.
- **Profitability corrected by its record.** `food-cost/cfo`'s `profitability` adds `projection_correction: {factor, bias_pct, note, prime_cost_pct, projected_prime_cost} | null` — the month-end prime-cost projection corrected by its own `profitability_month` record when earlier projections leaned one way ("already corrected because earlier forecasts here …"); null when the record shows no consistent lean. The raw figure is what is frozen and scored. It also adds `labor_basis` (`measured` — the nightly reports' labor dollars every night — `mixed`, or `analysis_share`) and `labor_basis_text` ("labor measured on 18 nights by your nightly reports, … for the rest"), which the morning brief's prime-cost line prints.

### Marketing (`/mobile/api/marketing/*`; web routes are mostly flat — `/api/mkt-stats`, `/api/mkt-performance`, `/api/mkt-insight`, `/api/content-calendar`, `/api/post-to-google`, `/api/brand-voice` (GET adds `can_edit`; POST is an account holder's only — 403 `owner_only` otherwise, re-audit PROMPTS-9; a field left out is kept), `/api/generate-content`, plus `/api/marketing/*` for drafts, schedule, links, media, attribution, diagnosis, tags)
Drafts (create/edit/list), media upload, scheduling, attribution per post, the text-club feature (`/api/guest-contacts*`, `/api/guest-campaign/draft|send`, `/api/guest-newsletter`, `/api/guest-qr`, `/api/guest-segments`, `/api/public/guest-optin/<token>`), templates.

Guest text consent (9/28/26):
- `POST /api/public/guest-optin/<token>` (public, the signed join link) — double opt-in: records a PENDING request (`guest_optin_requests`) and texts the number one confirmation ("{Restaurant}: Reply Y to confirm texts from us. Msg frequency varies. …", one GSM segment); marketing consent is set only by that phone's Y within 48 hours. Never touches consent, `last_visit` or `visit_count` and starts no review request. Answers `{ok: true, pending: true}` whether a text went, the number is already on the list or it once texted STOP (no text in those two); 429 past 3 submissions for one number a day (all restaurants) or 20 per IP an hour (durable, in the DB) beside the in-process 5 per IP per 5 minutes; 502 when the confirmation text could not be sent. `GET /join/<token>` renders `guest_marketing.JOIN_DISCLOSURE` word for word (its version and hash are recorded with each request) with /terms and /privacy links.
- `GET|POST /api/guest-optin-invites` (web twin of `/mobile/api/guest-optin-invites`) — the Toast review-link invite switch. GET `{ok, enabled, acknowledged_at, acknowledged_by, disclosure, disclosure_version}`; POST `{enabled, acknowledged}` — on needs `acknowledged: true` (400 otherwise), stores who and when; owner / client logins only (403 for a manager or member, or an admin). Both answers carry `can_change` (that rule, for this login — re-audit 10/8/26), so a client never offers the switch to a login it refuses. 403 without the Marketing module.
- `POST /webhooks/twilio/sms` passes Twilio's `MessageSid` to `handle_inbound_sms`, kept on the consent evidence. Inbound keywords are exact: only a message that IS START / UNSTOP / YES / Y undoes a STOP (granting no consent); only YES / Y answers a pending join confirmation (marketing consent) or review-link invite (review-link consent only, replied with the review link); a restaurant's name counts only as the answer to an open "which restaurant did you mean" question.
- `GET /api/guest-campaigns` `ledger` adds `review_only` and `pending`; `textable` is marketing consent only, and "this month" is the restaurant's calendar month.

Campaigns (9/28/26, the web's Marketing → Campaigns tab, formerly the guest text club):
- `GET /api/guest-overview` / `/mobile/api/guest-overview` — `guest_marketing.campaign_overview` plus `join_url` (the signed `/join/<id>-<sig>`; a bare `/join/<id>` is refused) and `receipt_hint`. Fields: `subscribers`, `today`, `last_30`, `weekly` (12 × `{week_start, joined}`, oldest first), `last_campaign` (`{date, sent, segment_label}` or null), `tap_rate` / `back_rate` (`{pct, campaigns}`, null until `rate_min` campaigns of 10+ texts carry the measurement), `delivered` (% this month or null), `texts_this_month`, `sending_now`, `window`, `min_days_between`, `rate_min`, `can_publish` (`marketing_drafts.may_publish` for this login — whether it may send, stop or retry; re-audit 10/8/26). No open rate (SMS reports none) and no revenue. 403 without the Marketing module.
- Marketing Opportunity Feed (9/28/26): `GET /api/marketing/opportunities[?show=all]` /
  `/mobile/api/marketing/opportunities[?show=all]` → `{ok, items, visible, sources, checked}`
  (`marketing_opportunities.feed`). Fields: `subscribers`, `today`, `last_30`, `weekly` (12 × `{week_start, joined}`, oldest first), `last_campaign` (`{date, sent, segment_label}` or null), `tap_rate` / `back_rate` (`{pct, campaigns}`, null until `rate_min` campaigns of 10+ texts carry the measurement; `back_rate` counts only campaigns whose whole 14-day window attribution has read — CS-8), `tap_by_segment` / `back_by_segment` (the same rates per audience key, an audience below the minimum absent — a Studio forecast uses the picked audience's own rate or none), `accepted` (% of this month's texts the carrier ACCEPTED, or null — it was `delivered`; there is no delivery receipt yet, CS-13), `texts_this_month`, `texts_failed_this_month`, `sending_now`, `window`, `min_days_between`, `rate_min`, `sms` (`{prefix, link_chars, link_example, stop_chars, max, sender}`: the "{Name}: " every campaign text opens with, a tracked link's length, the STOP line's, the 300 they all count against, and the number guests see it from — `TWILIO_GUEST_FROM_NUMBER`, display only, else `TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER` while guest texts ride the alert service; "" when unknown — the Studio's counter and phone preview). No open rate (SMS reports none) and no revenue. 403 without the Marketing module.
- `POST /api/guest-campaign/send` / `/mobile/api/guest-campaign/send` `{message, segment?, link_url?, type?, target_day?, hold?}` (fix round 9/28/26). Needs `MARKETING_APPROVE` (`marketing_drafts.may_publish`, as a post does) — 403 `CANNOT_PUBLISH` otherwise (CS-1). 400 before anything is queued or a link minted: `blocked: "unknown_segment"` (an audience that is no `SEGMENTS` key — it used to mean everyone, CS-17), `"too_long"` (the restaurant's name, the words and the link over 300), `"no_audience"` (nobody eligible — no campaign row is written, MB-11; `skipped_recent` says how many were texted in the last 3 days or are waiting on a campaign still sending). Outside 8am-9pm: `{ok: false, blocked: "quiet_hours"}` (200), unless `hold: true` — the Studio showed "Texts wait until 8:00 AM" — which queues it `waiting` for 8:00 AM. 202 `{ok, queued, campaign_id, total, skipped_recent, waiting, waiting_until, resumed, segment, segment_label}`: every recipient is a `guest_campaign_queue` row, texted by a background drain and resumed by the scheduler (`run_campaign_sends`), paced at `GUEST_SMS_PER_SECOND` (env, default 2) and each sent with Twilio's `ValidityPeriod` = seconds to 9 PM local, at most 4 hours (MB-13); every text opens with "{Name}: " (CS-12); a HELP reply names the restaurant and a contact (`SUPPORT_EMAIL`, else the operator address); the same words to the same audience while the first is still going resume it (`resumed`). `target_day` (a weekday, the Studio's fill-a-night plan) starts the slow-day tracker when the campaign finishes having sent something.
- `POST /api/guest-campaign/<id>/cancel` / `/mobile/api/guest-campaign/<id>/cancel` — stop a campaign still `sending` or `waiting`: its pending texts never go (`guest_marketing.cancel_campaign`). `MARKETING_APPROVE`. 200 `{ok, cancelled, status, sent, …}`, 404 for a campaign that isn't this restaurant's, 409 once it finished.
- `GET /api/guest-campaigns` rows add `status` (`sending` / `waiting` / `done` / `cancelled`), `total`, `pending`, `skipped_count`, `completed_at`, `target_day`, `waiting_until` and `window_closed` (its 14-day "came back" window fully read).
- `POST /api/guest-winback/<id>/send` takes `hold` too and needs `MARKETING_APPROVE`. `POST /api/guest-newsletter` needs `MARKETING_APPROVE`; a `mailing_address` other than the one on file is the account owner's to set (`permissions.is_principal`) — 403 `{owner_only: true}` for anyone else (MB-14).
- Marketing Opportunity Feed (9/28/26): `GET /api/marketing/opportunities` / `/mobile/api/marketing/opportunities`
  → `{ok, items, checked}` (`marketing_opportunities.feed`). Each item: `key` (a rec_ledger key — `slow_day:<Weekday>`,
  `holiday_promo:<ISO>`, `category_dip:<Category>`, `dish_promote:<Dish>`, `dish_praise:<Dish>`, `list_idle:text|email`,
  `post_this_week` / `first_post` — Home's posting keys), `rec_id` (null for a card not yet logged as shown), `kind`,
  `title`, `why`, `facts[]`, `stake` (`{amount, label}`, a measured gap, never an expected return, or null), `when`,
  `days_away`, `action` (`{prompt, channels}` for the Campaign Studio), `score`, `confidence` (K1, null on a fact card).
  Per viewer: a `dish_promote` card (a plate margin) only for a login with `FOOD_COST_VIEW` on a restaurant with the
  Food Cost module. Answered keys are left out BEFORE each kind is capped (two slow nights, two of each dish kind, one
  card per dish). Only the first `visible` (3) are logged as shown, with the confidence the page shows (K3); `?show=all`
  ("Show more") logs the rest. `sources`: `[{key, label, state: checked|no_data|failed, note}]` per read (a failure
  goes to `ops.capture` and that build is not stored); `checked` lists the labels read. Deterministic, stored per
  restaurant in `insight_cache` (kind `mkt_opps`) under a fingerprint of every input (connections, consent and
  unsubscribes, invite YESes, suppressions, processed dish reviews, finished sales nights, the category map, menu,
  recipes, ingredient costs, item sales, skipped holidays). 403 without the Marketing module.
  Studio sends from a card: `POST /api/guest-campaign/send`, `/api/guest-newsletter`, `/api/post-to-instagram`,
  `/api/post-to-facebook`, `/api/post-to-google` (and their mobile twins) take an optional `rec_key`; after a
  successful send it is recorded as `implemented` on that card's episode (`marketing_opportunities.implemented_by_send`
  — feed kinds only, an episode someone was shown).
- Campaign Studio (9/28/26): `GET /api/guest-overview` also returns `email_subscribers`, `mailing_address_set`,
  `insights` (`guest_marketing.campaign_insights`: at most three `{kind, figure, tone, text, basis}`, each measured —
  how many texted guests came back within 14 days over every campaign whose window has closed (one figure, no audience
  crowned: CS-8), the last email's opens as recorded — Apple Mail auto-opens included, never "at least"; never an
  estimate or money) and `last_campaign.channel` (`text` / `email`, the newer of the two). An email's
  `last_campaign.sent` is what was sent, never the recipient total.
  `GET /api/guest-segments` adds each segment's `email_count` (the same audience on the email list) and `eligible`
  (who a text would reach now: the three-day spacing and any campaign still sending taken out — the "Text N" count).
- `POST /api/guest-newsletter/draft` / `/mobile/api/guest-newsletter/draft` `{prompt, topic?}` →
  `guest_email.draft_newsletter`: `subject`, `preheader`, `headline`, `body`, `button_label`, `button_url` (the
  restaurant's `menu_url`, else ""), and the prompt's plan (`type`, `segment`, `goal`, `target_day`). 400 without a
  prompt, 422 with the reason when the guard refused the copy, 429 past 8 a minute. Nothing is sent.
- `POST /api/guest-newsletter/preview` / mobile twin `{subject, body, design}` → `{html, subject, body, preheader,
  image_url, mailing_address}`: the email exactly as a guest gets it (`guest_email.preview`, the send's own `_render`);
  `body` is the letter split from a raw Weekly email.
- `POST /api/guest-newsletter` also takes `design` (`guest_email.clean_design`: `headline`, `preheader`,
  `button_label`, `button_url` — http(s) only — and `image_media_id`, one of THIS restaurant's `marketing_media`; an
  image URL is never accepted) and `segment` (a `SEGMENTS` key; the email goes to that audience's subscribers). Both
  are part of the 24-hour same-newsletter check. The reply adds `segment`, and reports `sent`, `failed`,
  `retryable` (failures a retry can reach), `skipped` (unsubscribed / suppressed since it was recorded) and `queued`
  separately — never the total as sent; `resumed` when the press picked up an unfinished send. At most 3 go out
  inside the request (bounded at 5s), the scheduler tick sends the rest. Refused up front, nothing recorded, with no
  Resend key (`not_configured`). The same email again after it finished is a **409** `{already_sent, newsletter_id,
  sent_on (M/D/YY), new_subscribers, error: "Already sent on 9/28/26 — 3 new subscribers since."}` (fix round B,
  9/28/26). Recipients are one per address (lower-cased).
- `POST /api/guest-newsletter/<id>/retry` / `/mobile/api/guest-newsletter/<id>/retry` → `guest_email.retry_failed`:
  the failures a retry can fix (Resend 5xx/408/429, timeout, no key at the time) go back to pending and the first few
  are sent; never a rejected (4xx), suppressed, unsubscribed or interrupted-mid-send recipient. Idempotent (a second
  press retries 0). `{retried, sent, failed, retryable, skipped, total, queued}`; 404 for another restaurant's, 400
  without a key, 429 past 6 in 10 minutes.
- `POST /api/guest-newsletter/<id>/send-new` / mobile twin → `guest_email.send_to_new_subscribers`: the newsletter to
  the subscribers in its audience it never reached, added to the same newsletter; a second press adds nobody.
  `{added, sent, failed, …}`; 429 past 4 in 10 minutes. Offered only on the same-day `409 already_sent` answer (web
  and iOS); a newsletter older than `NEWSLETTER_SEND_NEW_HOURS` (25) is `409 {ok: false, too_old: true}` (re-audit
  10/8/26).
- `/e/<token>` (GET confirms, POST — the button or RFC 8058 one-click — unsubscribes): the token resolves to the
  address that email went to (`guest_newsletter_recipients.email_token`, else the contact's current one) and that
  ADDRESS is opted out of this restaurant's newsletters (`guest_email_optouts`), every contact row with it included.
- `GET /api/guest-newsletters` / mobile twin → `newsletters`: `guest_email.newsletter_history` (subject, body, design,
  `image_url`, `segment_label`, `total`, `sent`, `failed`, `retryable`, `skipped`, `pending`, and `opened` /
  `clicked` — as RECORDED: opens include Apple Mail auto-opens, a click on the unsubscribe link is not counted; null
  unless open tracking reports and the send kept its message id).
- `POST /api/guest-campaign/draft` (and its mobile twin) takes `prompt` (≤280 chars, the owner's goal): `guest_marketing.plan_campaign` picks the tone, audience and target day by keyword, and the reply adds `type`, `segment`, `goal`, `target_day`. A `type` sent with it wins (Rewrite keeps the tone). The prompt reaches the model as the goal, not as copy to include, and an offer it names may appear in the draft. A draft the guard refused answers **422** ("Cavnar AI didn't use that draft — <why>"); an empty or truncated reply, or any other failure, answers **500** "Couldn't draft a message right now — try again in a moment." (an empty reply used to come back as a finished, blank draft).

Client adoption (parity round, 9/25/26): iOS sends `type` with `POST /mobile/api/guest-campaign/send` (it labels the tracked link; the phone's sends all read "campaign"), and reads `GET /mobile/api/marketing/diagnosis` (strategy_routes, both prefixes) under the brief. Reviews: iOS calls `POST /mobile/api/templates` `{title, body}` ("Save as template") and `DELETE /mobile/api/templates/<id>`, and `GET /mobile/api/review-request-stats` in the request sheet; the web's review request sends the optional `message` (≤200 chars) `_do_send_review_request` always read.

One body per twin (parity round, 9/25/26):
- `POST /api/generate-content` and `/mobile/api/marketing/generate-content` → `client_api._do_generate_content`: `{ok, content, tags, validation, content_log_id}`, or `{ok: false, content: "", error}` with 429 (rate limit, budget stop) or `ai_utils.user_facing_error`'s status. The web route used to answer a rate limit with 200 and no `ok`. `content_log_id` (9/28/26, MB-8) is the generated row: a publish of these words sends it back so the post completes that row by id — the log used to attach a post to "the most recent unposted row for this topic". With `from_calendar: true` the topic is a calendar angle a model wrote, so it is never the owner's word for an offer, a price, a date or an event (`generate_content(topic_is_owner=False)`, AI-2), and a cost figure on a calendar card never reaches the post (`marketing.public_topic`, #88).
- Publishing (9/28/26, MB-8 / CS-20 / MB-21): `POST /api/post-to-instagram`, `/api/post-to-facebook`, `/api/post-to-google` (and the phone twins, which share the bodies) take optional `content_log_id` and `media_id` (a library photo, checked against the restaurant; the server builds its public URL — a relative `/m/<token>.jpg` `image_url` is made absolute too, and a `/m/<token>.jpg` URL — relative or on this origin — whose token is not this restaurant's photo is `400 "That photo isn't in your library."`, re-audit 10/8/26). Facebook with a photo posts to `/{page}/photos` (the answer's `post_id` is the Page post); Google carries it as the post's PHOTO media item. Every successful publish is logged with `posted_at`, topic or not (the caption's first 80 characters stand in). The phone's `/mobile/api/marketing/post-to-instagram` and `/post-to-facebook` resolve the photo through the same `social_routes.photo_url_from` as the web routes (iOS parity round 10/7/26, #6): a `media_id` from another restaurant is `400 {ok: false, error: "That photo isn't in your library."}` before Meta is called; the Facebook post with a photo goes to `/{page}/photos`, which the phone's twin used to drop. A second identical Google post inside 10 minutes is `409 {ok: false, duplicate: true, maybe_live: true}` (`gmb.post_local`), as Instagram and Facebook already were; a Google timeout, 5xx or 429 answers `{ok: false, maybe_live: true}` and the scheduled queue never retries it.
- `GET /api/post-insights` (9/28/26, MB-17) reads the recent posts' STORED metrics (`social_routes.stored_post_metrics`: `{ok, stored: true, posts: [{topic, post_id, platform, metrics, measured, metrics_synced_at}]}`) and never calls Meta — the web tab used to GET it every 60 seconds, each a Graph call per post on a request thread. `POST /api/post-insights` is the owner's "Refresh from Meta": rate limited 4 per 2 minutes on the phone's key (`mktmetrics:<rid>`, shared with `POST /mobile/api/marketing/refresh-metrics`; `{ok: false, throttled: true}` past it), bounded to 20 seconds, answering `refresh_post_metrics`' `{ok, status: ok|partial|failed, measured, attempted, refreshed, error}`.
- `GET /api/mkt-performance` / `/mobile/api/marketing/performance` and `GET /api/marketing/performance-window` / its twin (9/28/26, MB-6/7, AUX-13): `total_reach` / `reach` are reach alone over posts whose reach was measured (never reach + impressions; `total_impressions` / `impressions` ride separately); `engagement_rate` is engagements ÷ reach over those posts; `measured_posts` / `reach_posts` say how many posts the figures stand on (a client shows a dash, not 0, when there are none — the integer fields stay integers for the phone's decoder). `top_post` is `marketing_signals.best_posts`' top (most engagements, reach breaking a tie), null under `top_post_floor` (6) measured posts.
- `POST /api/marketing/links` and `/mobile/api/marketing/links` (9/28/26, MB-21): a short link is minted only to the restaurant's own hosts — its Menu URL's host and subdomains, its reservation system's host, and the platform — else `400 {ok: false, not_own_site: true, error}`. A public `/g/<token>` tap writes at most 60 times a minute per link and 10 per visitor; past that the guest is still forwarded, the tap is not written.
- Content types (`content_types` in `GET /mobile/api/marketing`, `ctypes` on the web) carry `channel`: `social`, `text` (`loyalty_nudge`) or `email` (`weekly_email`). Only a `social` type is published or scheduled: `marketing_publish.schedule_post` refuses a text or email type (`{ok: false, error: "… send it from Campaigns."}`), the web Content tab opens the Campaign Studio on that channel instead of drafting, and iOS `canPostSomewhere` is false for them (AUX-4).
- `POST /api/marketing/drafts/<id>/approve` (and the mobile twin) refuses a text or email draft — the quiet-night job's old `guest_sms` drafts, a saved Re-engagement text or Weekly email — with 403 `{ok: false, code: "send_from_campaigns", channel: "text"|"email", error}`: approving one used to flip its status and send nothing (AUX-5). The web drafts shelf offers "Open in Campaigns" for them instead of Approve.
- `GET /api/content-calendar` reads this week's cached calendar and never generates (`{ok: false, ideas: [], none_yet: true, error}` when there is none yet — the phone's `GET /mobile/api/marketing` reads the same way); `GET ?force=1` is a **405** (a GET is guarded by neither CSRF nor view-as-read-only, and it spent a paid draw). `POST /api/content-calendar` `{force?}` is Generate week — `force` defaults to true (a fresh draw); `{force: false}` builds this week's once when none is cached (the self-loading week, friction #41). It and `POST /mobile/api/marketing/calendar` → `mobile_api._do_content_calendar`: both mark `written` on ideas already written from (the web grid shows "Written from this idea"), both fall back to the week already cached with `stale: true` when a draw fails, both say why when there is none (`{ok: false, error}`, the `insight_error` status for an exception). The web reads `ideas`, the phone `calendar`; the web payload adds `ok`.
- `POST /api/post-to-google` and `/mobile/api/marketing/google-post` → `client_api._do_post_to_google`: not connected is `409 {ok: false, not_connected: true, error: "Connect Google Business first — Account → Connections."}` on both (the web said "Settings → Connections" with a 200, the phone 400); success is `{ok, post_id, name}`.
- `GET /api/mkt-performance` and `/mobile/api/marketing/performance` → `client_api._do_mkt_performance` (a failure is 500 on both).
- `POST /api/marketing/drafts` (and the mobile twin) takes `id` to update a saved draft; web and iOS now send it for a draft opened from the shelf or already saved, so a re-save no longer adds a copy. Opening a draft restores its content type and photo (`media_id`, `media_token` in the list). A save naming a draft that is gone answers 400 with `code: "draft_gone"`, one whose quiet night has passed `code: "draft_expired"`; the web composer then drops the id (a gone draft's words are saved as a new draft, an expired one waits for the owner's next Save).

Memory round (9/29/26) — what the owner's edits and results teach:
- **The model's draft is kept** (`marketing_voice`), so what went out is measured against it. `POST /api/generate-content` / `/mobile/api/marketing/generate-content` add `draft_ref`; `POST marketing/drafts` (both twins) takes it back beside `content_log_id` (a publish is found by its `content_log_id`). `POST /api/guest-campaign/draft` (+ mobile) and `POST /api/guest-newsletter/draft` (+ mobile) add `draft_ref`; `POST guest-campaign/send` and `POST guest-newsletter` take it back and record the words that went out against it.
- `POST /api/guest-campaign/draft` (+ mobile) adds `returns_by_segment: {segment: {label, back_per_100, campaigns, sent, came_back}}` (`guest_marketing.segment_returns`): guests who came back within 14 days per 100 texted, per audience, over closed-window campaigns of 10+ texts, only for an audience with 2 such campaigns (`CAMPAIGN_RATE_MIN`); `{}` until one clears it — "Haven't been in 60+ days: 9 came back per 100 texted (2 campaigns)".
- `GET /api/guest-campaigns` (+ mobile) rows add `night_effect: {date, lift_pct, text} | null` — what a fill-a-night campaign's own target night measured against a typical same weekday (`event_memory.campaign_night`, the one campaign measurement staffing and the forecast also read; "before and after, not proof"); null before that night is recorded or for a campaign aimed at no weekday.
- `GET /api/guest-newsletters` (+ mobile) rows add `results_as_of` once a newsletter's opens and clicks were stamped on the row 30 days after sending ("Opens and clicks as of M/D/YY"); `opened` / `clicked` then read the stamped figures, never a 0 from an aged-out log.
- `GET marketing/opportunities` items may carry `learned_weight` — the card's score was moved by this restaurant's own results for that key (`rec_learning.effectiveness`, reorder only; "Ranked by your results").

### Intel (`/mobile/api/intel/*`, `/api/intel*`, `/api/ai-visibility*`)
Competitor snapshots, AI-visibility run trigger + results.

`POST /api/refresh-competitor-intel` and `/mobile/api/intel/refresh-competitors` → `{ok, job_id}` (one body, `admin_routes.start_competitor_job`); the job's result, read by `GET /api/competitor-intel-status/<job_id>` and `/mobile/api/intel/refresh-status/<job_id>`, is the run's `{ok, competitors, insight, generated_at, closed_custom, discovered_at, custom_ids, …}`. Since the AI cost audit (10/7/26 #42) a read under six hours old, made with the owner-added competitors the restaurant has now, is that result as it stands — finished at once, no Places or model call — with `fresh: true`, `updated_at` and `note` ("Already up to date — refreshed 2h ago"), which the web shows as its toast. An admin may send `{"force": true}` on the web route to run anyway (audited as `competitor_intel.force_refresh`). A pressed Refresh searches the nearby set again; the scheduled runs re-read the stored place_ids until discovery is due (#39). `POST /api/intel/add-competitor` and `/mobile/api/intel/add-competitor` `{place_id}` answer 400 `{ok: false, error}` once ten owner-added competitors are tracked (`competitor.CUSTOM_COMPETITORS_MAX`, #40) — one the last read found temporarily closed is not counted, and one Google marks permanently closed is taken off the list by the next run (re-audit P7). `POST …/remove-competitor` also drops the place from the stored read's `custom_ids` and `closed_custom` when it is not in the comparison, and carries the same Full System plan gate as add (403; parity audit 10/7/26).

`/api/ai-visibility` and `/mobile/api/intel/ai-visibility` take **GET** (the recorded run: the six-hour process cache, else the last stored `ai_visibility_runs` row, `cached: true` + `measured_at`) and **POST** (9/26/26: the owner pressing Check / Re-run - every query runs live, `force=True`; the 3-per-minute limit and the budget ceiling still apply). Before this the buttons sent GET, so a check returned the stored run instantly. Ask's `read_ai_visibility` tool reads the GET path, and since the re-audit (10/7/26, P1) both Intel screens GET on open and render what comes back: a stored run (`state` `complete` | `partial`, `measured: true`, `measured_at`), or `state: "not_measured"`, `measured: false`, `ai_score: null` and a `reason` — shown as "Not measured yet", never as 0. A POST that got no answer at all is `{ok: false, error, state: "not_measured", measured: false, partial: false, answered_queries: 0}` (P8: it was ok, partial and measured); the clients keep the last recorded check on screen. A POST that sends `async` (JSON body, parity audit 10/7/26 #75 — both the web and the phone send it) starts the check on the owner AI job pool (`ai_async`, kind `ai_visibility`, 150 s) and answers `202 {ok, async, status: "pending", job_id, joined, wait_seconds}`; the client polls `/ai-jobs/<job_id>` for the payload above. Without the flag the POST answers in the request, as before; a GET is never a job. `GET /api/intel/ai-visibility/queries` (and the mobile twin) is each question across the last checks — the phone now draws its dot strip too.

`GET /api/intel/movement` and `/mobile/api/intel/movement` (one body, `mobile_api.mobile_intel_movement`): `?days` (default 90, max 365) → `{ok, days, movement[], significant[], arrived: [{place_id, name}], gone: [...], compared_from, compared_to, claim_kinds}` — `arrived`/`gone` compare the last two weekly snapshot dates (`compared_from`/`compared_to` are ISO dates, `null` until two exist); `significant` is the rating moves past the review-volume noise floor (`confidence_z` ≥ 2). Both Intel screens render it as "What changed" under Nearby competitors (parity round, 9/25/26); before two checks it says it needs two to compare, never "no change".

Since the memory round (9/29/26) the same payload carries the market's kept history (`event_memory`, never pruned): `market_history: [{place_id, name, kind: arrived | gone | rating_up | rating_down, from_rating, to_rating, review_count, observed_on}]` (newest first, at most `mobile_api.MARKET_HISTORY_MAX` = 30; `observed_on` is ISO — clients print M/D/YY) and `own_rating_history: {available, first: {week, rating, review_count}, latest, change, weeks, series}` — the restaurant's own public rating week by week, oldest first. Both are `measured` in `claim_kinds`.

### Ask Cavnar (`/mobile/api/ask-cavnar/*`, `/api/ask-cavnar*`)
`opening` (deterministic briefing, no model call), `conversations` (list/create), `history` (a conversation's messages), the streaming chat endpoint itself (SSE), and the tool-confirmation route a `write`-kind tool's proposal card posts to. Each proposal in an answer carries `proposal_id`, `details` (`[{label, value}]`), `preview` (the words that would go out), `at_stake` (dollars, or null) and `fields_shown` (`[{key, label, value}]` — every field of `body`; NS5 C1). `body` holds only the fields in the tool's own input schema, never one of `ask_cavnar_tools.PROPOSAL_DENYLIST` (`acknowledge`, `resend`, `draft_hash` from the model, `earned`, `include_4star`, `paused`, …), and a proposal whose link (a `link_url`, or a URL inside a message or caption) is off the restaurant's own domains (`menu_url`, logo host, Cavnar's own links) is not built — the model is told why (`proposal_refusal`). The words themselves are checked too (AUX-2 / #15, `_public_copy_refusal`): a `send_guest_campaign` message on the guest_sms surface and a `publish_*` caption on social_post, with the restaurant's profile, menu notes and the owner's own question (`build_proposal(…, owner_words=question)`) as the offer source — an offer, price, date, time or event the owner didn't say, a new/back/better/changed claim, an invented sign-off, never-say, an award, over 300 characters for a text, or a draft the engine would reword builds no card. `send_guest_campaign` requires `segment` (`all`, `lapsed_30`, `lapsed_60`, `regulars`, `new` — `guest_marketing.SEGMENTS`; no silent "all"); the card's Recipients row counts that segment and shows its label. Web and iOS render every `fields_shown` row under "Sent with this" and post only those keys. The `publish_schedule` card lists the current blockers ("Needs a look first"); the `approve_all_reviews` card lists each reply that would post and how many the reply check would hold; `POST ask-cavnar/action` takes `{action, outcome, proposal_id?, reason?, summary, conversation_id}` — a `proposal_id` not this restaurant's (or naming another action), or proposed to another login when the caller is not an account principal, is a 404 (re-audit B6); the audit line quotes the proposal's own summary (a client summary is capped at 200 characters, a `body` over 4,000 is dropped) and lands in `conversation_id` only when that chat is the caller's own. A turn saved without a conversation lands in the CALLER's current chat, never another login's (B5); `conversations` previews and counts only the turns the caller may read, and `DELETE ask-cavnar/history` clears only the caller's chats.

**Screen context (friction #15).** `POST /api/ask-cavnar`, `/api/ask-cavnar/stream` and both `/mobile/api` twins take an optional `screen: {panel, entity: {type, id}}` — where the asker is: `panel` one of home | reviews | labor | inventory | marketing | competitor | intel | account | dsr | recs; `entity.type` review (an integer id) or rec (a key-shaped string). The server builds the prompt line itself (`ask_cavnar.screen_hint`): a review is looked up in the login's own restaurant and described by its rating, platform, date and reply status only — never its author or text — and anything unrecognised is dropped. The question stays the only instruction. The web sends it with every question; a review card's and an Intel recommendation's "Ask about this" set the entity.

**View-as is support's own thread (memory re-audit 9/29/26, PEOPLE-7 / PEOPLE-20).** Every Ask route (web and mobile — ask, stream, history, conversations, action, feedback, opening's tally) reads and writes under `client_api._ask_uid(current_user)` (`permissions.acting_login_id`): the admin behind a view-as session, never the owner it views as. A view-as chat is filed under the admin and marked `via = 'view_as'`; the owner's history, notes, "often asks" and past chats never include it, and support never lists or opens the owner's chats. A rating through view-as is the admin's own row (`POST ask-cavnar/feedback` answers 404 for an answer that is not support's). Reading `account/memory`, `ask-cavnar/history` or `ask-cavnar/conversations[/<id>]` through view-as is recorded (`admin_events` `view_as_read`). An `ask-cavnar/action` confirm/dismiss records its `authority` (`ask_cavnar_actions.authority`, `acting_admin_id`) and passes it to the ledger.

**New chat is lazy on both clients (9/25/26).** Neither client calls `POST ask-cavnar/conversations` any more (the route stays for older builds): "New chat" clears the screen and the first question carries `new_conversation: true`, so the server creates the chat with that question. `GET ask-cavnar/conversations` (both twins, `models.list_ask_conversations`) no longer lists a chat with no turns the caller may read — an untouched New chat, or one whose first question failed after the row was made. The web's plain-JSON fallback (`POST /api/ask-cavnar`, used only when the stream never started) now posts the stream's own body — `{question, conversation_id, new_conversation, screen}`, no client `history` — so its answer lands in the same chat and replays the same stored turns. An answer with `truncated: true` shows "Answer was cut short" under it on the web as on iOS.

**Memory round (9/29/26) — Ask.** Two new confirm cards turn a constraint the owner states into the store the schedule obeys, instead of a sentence in memory: `add_closed_date` `{date}` ("Mark {day} as closed" — posts `account-settings/closures` / mobile `account/closures`; an account holder's tool only) and `set_staff_unavailable` `{employee_name, weekdays[]}` (posts `labor/availability`; the person's other blocked days and notes are kept). Both render through `fields_shown` like any card. The answer's meta (`client_api._ask_meta`, both streams' `answer` event and the JSON fallback) adds `declined_repeats: [{text, signature, declined_on (M/D/YY)}]` — advice in the prose the owner already said "not for us" to on some surface, kept but caveated in place ("(you passed on this on 8/12/26)"; `decisions.annotate_declined`); `[]` when none. A proposal card's `fields_shown` values print an ISO date as M/D/YY (`ask_cavnar_tools._shown`); the `body` keeps ISO.

### Connections (`/mobile/api/connections/*`)
Per-integration connect/disconnect/status: Google Business, Instagram, Toast, Square, Clover.

**Website analytics** (10/2/26, `web_analytics`): `GET /api/web-analytics` (any console login: `configured`, `service_email` — the address to add in GA4 / Search Console — `ga4_property_id`, `gsc_site_url`, `synced_at`, `error`, `can_edit`); `POST /api/web-analytics` `{ga4_property_id, gsc_site_url}` (owner-only; 400 for a G- measurement ID or a bare domain; each part checked with Google, `checks: {ga4|gsc: {ok, error, code}}`, `syncing` when the first read started); `POST /api/web-analytics/disconnect` (owner-only); `POST /api/web-analytics/sync` (read now, once per 10 minutes — the web's connect card and the phone's Intel card both have Read now since 10/7/26); `GET /api/marketing/website` (Marketing-gated: `totals` vs the window before, `line`, `channels`, `clicks`, `queries`, `signals` with `context`, `signals_basis`). Phone twins under `/mobile/api/web-analytics…` and `/mobile/api/marketing/website`, one body each.
**Sync now and RPOWER disconnect** (iOS parity #80, 10/7/26): `POST /mobile/api/connections/<toast|square|clover|rpower>/sync` — the web card's `/api/<pos>/sync`, on the same body (`<pos>_routes.do_sync`): any login, the manual-sync path the console and the nightly sync use; `{ok, message[, job_id]}`, `{ok: false, error}` when that POS isn't connected (RPOWER answers that with 400, as on the web), 404 for another name. `DELETE /mobile/api/connections/rpower` — `/api/rpower/disconnect`'s body (`rpower_routes.do_disconnect`), principal only; connecting again is Cavnar AI's.

**Website analytics** (10/2/26, `web_analytics`): `GET /api/web-analytics` (any console login: `configured`, `service_email` — the address to add in GA4 / Search Console — `ga4_property_id`, `gsc_site_url`, `synced_at`, `error`, `can_edit`); `POST /api/web-analytics` `{ga4_property_id, gsc_site_url}` (owner-only; 400 for a G- measurement ID or a bare domain; each part checked with Google, `checks: {ga4|gsc: {ok, error, code}}`, `syncing` when the first read started); `POST /api/web-analytics/disconnect` (owner-only); `POST /api/web-analytics/sync` (read now, once per 10 minutes); `GET /api/marketing/website` (Marketing-gated: `totals` vs the window before, `line`, `channels`, `clicks`, `queries`, `signals` with `context`, `signals_basis`). Phone twins under `/mobile/api/web-analytics…` and `/mobile/api/marketing/website`, one body each.

**Re-audit of Reviews / Intel (10/8/26).** `POST /approve/<id>` and `POST /mobile/api/reviews/<id>/approve` take `approve_skipped: true` — sent only by "Approve after all" on the web card and the phone's review screen; without it a skipped reply is refused `409 {ok: false, skipped: true, error}` (a queued offline replay, the lock-screen action, a bell row, Ask, the rule and a bulk publish never overturn a skip; `models.claim_approval(approve_skipped=…)`). The web's Approve sends the card's reply as `expected_draft`, and Save & approve the words just saved. A review push (`push._draft_fields`) carries `draft_hash` (`models.draft_hash`) beside `draft` / `draft_complete`, kept when the draft is clipped or dropped to fit; the lock-screen Approve & post sends it as `expected_draft_hash` (a push with neither text nor hash only opens the review). Ask's `approve_review` card body adds `expected_draft_hash`; `approve_all_reviews` adds `review_hashes` (`{review id: draft_hash}`) beside `review_ids`. `POST /api/reviews/approve-all` (and the mobile twin) take `review_hashes`: each listed reply posts only while it still hashes to it (one with no fingerprint does not post), and the answer adds `changed` (rewritten since the card, not posted); the phone's Select confirm lists the replies and sends them. Inbox rows carry `post_attempted_at` and `post_error`; `post_failed` is false while a post is in flight (`models.review_post_in_flight`). `GET /api/web-analytics` (and twin) adds `can_sync` (the Read now gate, MARKETING_APPROVE). `GET /api/intel/website` and `/mobile/api/intel/website` — the website summary (`_do_marketing_website`), gated as Intel, for the phone's card under Intel.

**Instagram & Facebook connect** (10/2/26): one Meta sign-in (`GET /instagram/connect`, owner-only; mobile `GET /mobile/api/connections/instagram/authorize`) binds the restaurant's Facebook Page and the Instagram business account linked to that Page — `me/accounts` with `instagram_business_account{id,username}`. One Page shared binds it; several show a picker in the popup (each Page by name with its Instagram) that posts `POST /instagram/choose` (`pick` + `page`, CSRF-exempt: `pick` is the restaurant and the sign-in sealed with Fernet under a key derived from SECRET_KEY, 15 minutes — nothing is stored before the choice). A Page with no Instagram linked connects Facebook alone and clears any old Instagram. The popup answers `{ig:'connected', page, username, instagram}` to its own origin, or `{ig:'error', msg}` with msg in denied / no_code / state_invalid / token_failed / pages_failed / no_pages / page_gone / save_failed.
