Each token's expiry is Meta's own (`debug_token`); a never-expiring Page token stores none and is never refreshed. `fb_page_name` and `ig_username` name what was bound on the Connections card.

`GET /mobile/api/account` → `connections` (CA3 F6/F13): `toast`, `square`, `clover` and now `rpower` each `{connected, last_synced, error, sync_state, age_days}` — `sync_state` from `pos_health.provider_state` (current | aging | stale | error | unknown | not_connected); `pos` is `pos_health.pos_sync_state` for the provider in use; `google_business` adds `source` (gbp | places_sampled | none) and `label` ("Google reviews (sampled — Places returns 5 at a time)" for Places-only). All new keys are additive; clients decode them optional.

`POST /mobile/api/connections/toast` and the web twins (`/api/toast/save`, `/admin/toast/save/<id>`) refuse the literal "demo" credentials unless the restaurant is flagged `is_demo` (CA3 F8).

An owner's own connect — `POST /api/toast/save`, `/api/square/save`, `/api/clover/save` and `POST /mobile/api/connections/toast`, `/square`, `/clover` — refuses **409** a store another live restaurant already holds, with a sentence that never names that restaurant (`models.owner_pos_binding_refusal`; a demo restaurant is exempt). The owner's "Sync now" (`POST /api/<toast|square|clover|rpower>/sync`) runs on the admin task pool (`scheduler.start_manual_pos_sync`); Square and Clover answer `{ok, job_id, message}`, Toast and RPOWER `{ok, message}`.

Data Freshness fix round, workstream J (9/24/26) — additive: `GET /health` adds `jobs_overdue` (job names past `ops.EXPECTED_JOBS`; any makes `status` "degraded", still 200). Admin `client`/overview records add `data_health` `{overall, as_of, failing: [{source, provider, failures, since, error, next_retry_at, last_ok_at}]}` (the newest `data_health_daily` row plus live `source_health` failures); overview `kpis` add `jobs_overdue`, `data_health_median`, `sources_failing`, and an overdue job is a platform issue (`job_overdue:<job>`). `POST /admin/api/jobs/<name>/run` refuses while a run of that job is unfinished and under 4 hours old (was 30 minutes) or running in-process. `RUNNABLE_JOBS` adds `pos_retry` and `data_health_daily`. The freshness registry adds a `depletion` source (in `inventory`, `food`, `food_cost`, `ordering`), a `disconnected` POS state, weather `unknown` when never fetched but possible, and `sales`/`labor` errors inherited from a failing POS or from final daily reports disagreeing with the POS on 2+ nights.

Admin: `POST /admin/api/client/<id>/delete-demo` (step-up) `{confirm_name}` permanently deletes a restaurant flagged `is_demo` and every row that belongs to it (`models.delete_restaurant`); 400 unless it is a demo and the name matches exactly. `/admin/api/client/<id>` → `client.integrations[]` lists `rpower` beside toast/square/clover, each POS row with `sync_state`/`age_days` and an `error` when a sync simply stopped (stale, no error written); `client.freshness.pos_state` is `pos_health.pos_sync_state`; `client.freshness.inventory` is the newest `ingredients.last_recount_at`; `client.setup_completeness` `{score, checks, label: "Setup completeness"}` — `data_completeness` is the same object, kept as an alias until admin.html reads the new key.

### Home (`/api/home/brief`, `/api/home/brief/group`, `/mobile/api/home`, `/mobile/api/home/brief/group`, `/mobile/api/home/modules`)
The deterministic Home-tab payload — see `home_brief.py`. `?fresh=1` forces recompute past the 60s per-restaurant cache. `/group` returns every location in an owner's `location_group` side by side; each of its `attention[]` rows adds `nav` (the nav path "Open" lands on after switching to that location — a reviews issue opens the inbox on `filter=urgent|pending`) and `action_label` (friction #24).

**Web / iOS parity round (9/25/26) — additive.** `/mobile/api/home` now carries every top-level key of `/api/home/brief` (`mobile_api._brief_passthrough`): `quick_actions`, `snapshot`, `charts`, `changes`, `dismissed`, `upcoming`, `alerts`, `ask_suggestions`, `ai_insight`, `context` (with `locations`), `greeting_name`, `generated_at`, `empty_state`, and `brief` is now the whole brief object (`headline`, `tone`, `lines`, `counts`, `overnight`, `data_as_of` — older builds read only the first two). The phone keeps its own shapes for `needs_attention` (the brief's `attention`) and `weekly_receipts` (the brief's `receipts`) — `mobile_api.MOBILE_HOME_ALIASES`; `tests/test_home_parity.py` fails when a brief key is missing from the phone's payload. `GET /mobile/api/home/brief/group` is the twin of `/api/home/brief/group` (one body, `home_brief.build_group_brief`: `LOCATION_SWITCH` only — 403 otherwise — and only the login's own group, `location_group` AND `owner_email`). `GET /mobile/api/notifications` takes `mark=0` (read without moving the read mark; a row is read when opened, `POST notifications/opened` with its `alert_id`) and `scope=group`, and `GET /mobile/api/notifications/unread-count` takes `scope=group` — one body with the web routes (`client_api._do_read_notifications` / `_do_notifications_unread`); without them an older build keeps "reading the list reads it all". Every approve path — web and mobile approve-all, a single approve, Ask's confirmed `approve_all_reviews`, the scheduler's auto-approve — drops the restaurant's cached Home inside the shared body (`client_api._invalidate_home`, after the writes). The `dsr` list row adds `vs_yesterday_pct` (net against the night before, when the Sales block is ready) so the phone's Last night card reads one call.

A morning-brief line with a direct action (`GET morning-brief` → `brief.lines[]`, the brief email) adds `action: {label, nav}` (friction #46, `morning_brief.line_action`): `reviews` → `reviews?filter=pending` ("Answer them") or `reviews?filter=urgent` ("Reply now"), `stock` → `inventory/order`, `schedule` → `labor/schedule`, `issues` → `home`. The email's link is `/?nav=<path>`.

**Recommendation Confidence — contracts K1/K4** (confidence audit, 9/24/26; `rec_trust.assess`, see `INTELLIGENCE_ENGINE.md` → Recommendation Confidence). Every Home card (`recommendations[]`) and attention item (`attention[]`; mobile `needs_attention[]`) carries `confidence: {pct (0-100 | null), band (low|medium|high, derived), label ("72% confidence" | "Confidence not yet measurable"), reason, score (pct/100, 0.0 when null — iOS decodes it non-optional), caution, dimensions: {evidence: {pct, basis, n, kind}, accuracy: {pct|null, basis, n, improved, source: own|cohort|none, low, high}, freshness: {pct|null, basis, as_of (M/D/YY), as_of_iso, stalest}}, version: 1}`. Cards no longer carry `strength` (clients must tolerate it missing); a card with dollars carries `dollars_basis` (the figure's scope); the labor-over attention item carries `dollars_weekly` + `dollars_basis`; a POS sync failure (any provider, RPOWER included) is the critical attention `pos_sync` with `provider`. `freshness[]` entries are `{module, source, state (current|aging|stale|not_connected|unknown|sample), pct, as_of, as_of_iso, basis, error}` plus the legacy `key/label/at/note`; `brief.data_as_of` is the stalest source's M/D/YY date; `monitoring: {count_live (current sources only), sources, stale (sources reading stale or unknown), all_clear (nothing needs the owner, ≥ 1 source current and none stale — draw an "All clear" box only when true), stalest_as_of}`; the headline never reads "Running well" over stale data. The labor `snapshot` tile carries `below_floor` (under `labor.MIN_DAYS_TO_EXTRAPOLATE` days: value "—" and what is needed) and `stale` (the labor sources under 50%: neutral state, "Out of date" interpretation); the labor brief line and the `labor_on_target` win follow the same floors. `/mobile/api/labor` carries `labor_freshness: {pct, state, stale, basis, as_of, as_of_iso}`, `staffing_board` (`labor.staffing_board`: `{overstaffed[], lean[], overtime[], summary}` — the web Labor tab's decision cards, null on sample data; `summary.at_stake` = `summary.over` (straight-time labor above target on the overstaffed days) + `summary.ot` (the whole overtime premium), two parts that do not overlap; on the assumed wage (`thresholds.labor_cost_basis == "default"`) `summary.dollars_withheld: "default_rate"`, `at_stake`/`over` null, their texts "—", `withheld_text` the line both clients draw, and each overstaffed card `withheld: true` with no dollars or trim hours — the overtime premium stays, as on the savings tiles), `money_went` (`labor.money_went`, ranked; `[]` on sample data; on the assumed wage the overstaffed and past-schedule items are left out) and `blended_rate` (the registry's reading of the labor sources; null on sample data). `/mobile/api/home` carries the same `freshness`, `monitoring`, `data_as_of`. The one-thing hero (`GET /cross-module` → `fix_first`) carries `confidence` (K1), `claim_kind`, `model_written`, and `dollars_basis` when it has dollars; the morning brief's `money` line carries `money: {low, high, label}` so a range is never collapsed to one figure. Where an older client decodes a field named `confidence` as a string — review / food / labor / campaign diagnosis blocks, food cost drivers (and the food brief's `fix_first`), the Reviews read's "Do today" rec — `confidence` stays the band word and the K1 object is `confidence_detail` (drivers also carry `evidence_input`).

**Recommendation Confidence — the support score (round 2, group P, 9/24/26) — additive.** The overall `pct` means how well SUPPORTED the advice is (evidence × track record × freshness), never the chance it works (`INTELLIGENCE_ENGINE.md` → Recommendation Confidence). Every K1 object adds: `meaning` ("How well supported this is — not the chance it works" — clients lead the Why? panel with it), `thresholds: {high: 75, medium: 50}` (read the band cut-offs from here), `caps: {no_track_record: 70, record_unproven: 70, record_against: 49, stale: 49, stale_below: 50, freshness_unmeasured: 49, beats_at: 95, against_below: 50, max: 99}`, `caps_applied` (the ceilings that set the figure, in the engine's order: `no_track_record`, `record_unproven`, `record_against`, `stale`, `freshness_unmeasured`; `reason` names the last one that bound), `sample` (bool — sample data: say "not yet measurable", never "0%"); `version` is 2. `dimensions.evidence` adds `n_full` (what a full sample is for its kind) and `corroborating` (other modules whose verified figures agree, 0–2); new evidence kinds `nights` (DSR actions: nights of observation behind the traced figures), `supplier_quotes`, `recounts`, `recipe_lines`. `dimensions.accuracy` is now how likely the kind beats doing nothing here: `pct` = round(100 × P), `basis` the lift sentence ("improved 4 of 6 times vs 1 of 6 when not acted on" / "vs about 5% by chance"), adds `p_beats`, `beats_label` ("93% likely to beat doing nothing"), `lift: {improved, n, rate, untaken_improved, untaken_n, do_nothing_rate, source: untaken|false_alarm|stated}` and `prior: {source: do_nothing|cohort, centre, weight, cohort_measured, cohort_improved}`; a cohort record alone never carries a `pct`. `dimensions.freshness` adds `stalest_basis` and `errors` (sources whose sync failed). **Facts carry no confidence:** Home attention items and cards in `home_brief.HOME_FACT_KEYS` (setup/health nudges, `pos_sync`, `urgent_reviews`, `stale_low_reviews`, `awaiting_approval`, `low_response_rate`, `critical_low`, `overtime`, `publish_drafts`) carry `confidence: null`, as do the hero's `urgent_reviews` and the group view's counts; the morning brief's running-low line carries none. Shift Quality (`quality` on the schedule payloads) adds `confidence_detail` — the panel's own K1 — beside each `recommendation_items[].confidence`. `/mobile/api/home` `needs_attention[]` adds `dollars_weekly` and `dollars_basis`. Owner-added competitors carry `rating_is_provisional` like discovered ones.

**Calibrated dollars and "not for us" everywhere** (confidence audit integration, F6 + H16, 9/24/26). Every Home card (`recommendations[]`), the one-thing hero (`fix_first`) and every DSR action (`actions_tomorrow[]`) that carries `dollars_monthly` also carries, from `rec_learning.attach_dollar_calibration`: `dollars_adjusted` (the figure times this restaurant's measured realised-÷-shown ratio for the kind — median of its pairs, shrunk toward 1 by 3 pseudo-pairs, bounded 0.8–1.2, and from `CALIBRATION_WIDE_PAIRS` = 8 pairs 0–1.2 — or `null` below `rec_learning.MIN_CALIBRATION_PAIRS` = 3 measured results), `calibration_n` (the measured pairs behind it, 0 when none), `calibration_note` ("adjusted from N measured results", or `null`) and `realised_mean` (additive, confidence re-audit B2 #8: the mean monthly dollars those measured results actually realised, a no-clear-change result counting $0 — `null` below 3 pairs; show it beside the estimate). **Display rule:** show `dollars_adjusted` with `calibration_note` beneath it when it is non-null, else `dollars_monthly`; the raw `dollars_monthly` stays in the payload and is what the ledger snapshots as `dollar_value` (the ratio is realised ÷ dollar_value, so snapshotting the adjusted figure would feed each correction into the next). Ranking still reads the raw figure times the learned weight. Every Home card, attention item and the hero also carry `advice_signature` (`"<family>:<subject>"`, e.g. `labor:day:tuesday`, or `null` — `insight_store.advice_signature(key, title)`): an item whose signature is in `insight_store.declined_signatures(rid)` — a "not for us" on ANY surface, still in force — is left out server-side (a critical attention item never is), the same filter the DSR actions and the Reviews "Do today" line apply.

**How you compare** (Benchmarking audit, workstream O, 9/24/26) — built only from the Benchmark Engine (`GET /api/benchmarks`), shaped by `benchmark_views`. `GET /api/benchmarks/card?module=labor|food_cost|reviews|marketing` (and `?module=home` for Home's strip) + `/mobile/api/benchmarks/card`: `{ok, scope: module|home, module, who: {kind: peers|self|industry|null, text ("Compared to 11 other Pizza restaurants on Cavnar" / "vs your own previous 13 weeks" / "published: NRA 2025 …"), n, as_of (M/D/YY), inferred}, strength: {pct, label "68% comparison strength", reason, meaning, caps_applied, rows: [{key: size|freshness|own|similarity, title, pct, basis}], footer} | null (only for a band comparison), rows: [{metric, label, unit, module, kind, value, value_text, standing, tone good|neutral|warn (never red), behind, against, middle_text, as_of, strength_pct, note (labor vs a published figure: "includes benefits; yours is wages from your shifts"), action: {label, ask} | null (one per metric it is behind on), open_module (home only)}], below_minimum: {text "Not enough restaurants like yours yet — here's how you compare to your own last 13 weeks.", why_not (the engine's)} | null, unmeasured, behind, empty}` — projected by the login's module view permissions (400 "This login can't see that module."). `GET /api/benchmarks/locations` + `/mobile/api/benchmarks/locations` (and `location_compare` inside `/api/home/brief/group`): `{ok, metrics: [{metric, label, unit, better, locations: [{id, name, value, value_text, this, vs_own ("about your normal" | … | null without 6 weeks of history), vs_group ("ahead of / behind / in line with your other locations" | "not called — needs 6 weeks of history to tell a gap from noise"), tone, called, group_median_text}]}], why_not}` — the engine's `location` kind (the `privacy.org_key` organization, each location by its `location_name`, like for like: a format or economics metric only between locations in the same confirmed partition, and never a $26/hr-default labor cost or an irregularly logged waste %; own data, `LOCATION_SWITCH` logins only, closed without a viewer); a gap is called only beyond the location's own noise band and the others' median one combined. The group brief's `portfolio.strongest` / `weakest` come from `benchmark_views.rank_by_rating` (each `{location, id, rating, reviews, vs_own}`): a rating needs `thresholds.GROUP_RANK_MIN_REVIEWS` (now `RATING_MIN_REVIEWS`, 5) and the two must differ by more than 2 standard errors, else both are null and `portfolio.ranking_basis` says why; `locations[].reviews` adds `vs_own`. `/mobile/api/food-cost/menu-profitability` (and its web twin) adds `priced[].cost_tone` (good|warn|bad|neutral) and `cost_reference: {pct, kind: target|published|rule_of_thumb|vendor|starting_target, basis, target_label, target_source} | null` (`cogs.dish_reference` / `dish_tone` — the owner's food-cost target, else the top of a published band for the typengine calls `comparable` (none today), else Cavnar's starting target, where a dish tops out at `warn`; re-audit #2, #10). The food-cost analysis adds `waste_target: {pct, kind: yours|starting, basis}`; `benchmark_label` is "Under target / Near target / Over target / Well over target" (no longer "Excellent / On Track / Above Average / Concerning / Needs Attention"). `/api/food-cost/cogs` `benchmark` adds `source_kind`, `band_name`, `comparable` and `definition_note`, and `label` names the kind ("Within the industry band (NRA 2025)", "Above the rule-of-thumb band", "… vendor guidance") — only for a band the engine calls `comparable`; otherwise the band is context and, with no target of the owner's, `label` is "Under / Above Cavnar's starting target" (tone at most `warn`). The payload adds `food_cost_target_label` ("your target" | "Cavnar's starting target") and `food_cost_target_source` (set | seeded | default) from `thresholds.target_for` (re-audit #2, #10). K1 `dimensions.accuracy` adds `cohort_label` when a cohort stood in. Re-audit (workstream O, 9/25/26): each row adds `standing` in outcome words ("in the group's worst quarter — higher than 3 in 4"; null on a context or stale row) with `standing_key` (the engine's word), `context` (a published figure measured differently — the engine's `definition_note`, no standing, tone or action, never on the Home strip), `stale` (the metric's data_freshness source is stale — "Last measured M/D/YY", standing withheld), `own_as_of` (the restaurant's own figure, M/D/YY) and `tag` ("vs 9 like yours · 80% comparison strength" — the row's own group); `who.kind` is `mixed` (with `groups`) when rows come from different groups; the empty state adds `reason` (unconfirmed|concept|wage|spread|owners|own_data|few) and `action: {kind: "profile", label "Confirm your profile", suggestion, can_edit} | null`. `/mobile/api/food-cost` (and the analytics `cogs` block) carries `food_cost_target_label` / `food_cost_target_source` (filled by `mobile_api.food_target_labelled` when the body lacks them); `/mobile/api/food-cost/trend` `target` adds `label` ("your target" | "Cavnar's starting target"); the web Labor tab's `savings_breakdown` adds `labor_target_label` / `labor_target_source`.

**Data Health on the owner surfaces** (Data Freshness audit, workstream O, 9/24/26) — additive. `GET /api/data-health` + `/mobile/api/data-health` (`data_health.snapshot`) `sources[]` add `pending` (connected, first sync not landed — never scored; `overall` reads `{pct: null, state: "pending", label: "Waiting for the first sync"}` when nothing else is in use), `expected_by` (ISO-Z, the next scheduler slot — `data_health.SLOTS`, the scheduler's own Chicago times), `expected_line` ("POS sync runs tonight 3am", restaurant clock), `last_sync_local` ("3:02am" / "9/21/26 · 3:02am"), `counts_current` (current AND last success inside one cadence + 2h — the only sources any surface may call current/live) and `can_sync_now`; the payload adds `count_current` and `connections: {pos, google}` (`data_health.connection_lines`: `{line, tone ok|warn|bad|off, state, last_sync, data_through, next, …}` — "Last sync 3:02am · Sales through 9/19/26", "Checked 11:02am · next check 4pm", "Last check 9/21/26 — 6 checks missed"; restaurant-local, never UTC, never the sync-run stamp). `/api/home/brief` and `/mobile/api/home` carry `data_health: {overall, worst_line}` beside the legacy `freshness[]`; `monitoring.count_live` counts only `counts_current` sources (the web says "N current sources"); `/mobile/api/home` adds `freshness_unavailable` (true when the brief failed — say "Couldn't check how current your data is"). Home's `pos_sync` attention item also fires on a registry-stale POS (sales stopped arriving), "important", not only on a sync error. `/mobile/api/account` `connections` adds `pos_line`, `google_business.fetch_line` and, on the chosen provider row, `sync_line`/`sync_tone`; `/mobile/api/reviews` (first page) adds `fetch_line`; `/api/mkt-performance`, `/mobile/api/marketing` and `/mobile/api/marketing/performance` add `metrics_sync: {line: "Metrics synced 9/21/26", tone ok|warn, synced_at, state, error}` (`data_health.metrics_line`). Every K1 confidence object adds `confidence_impact` (`confidence_engine.freshness_impact` — `{now, when_current, delta, blocked_by, basis}`, null under a 3-point move) for the Why? drawer's "94% once inventory counts are current". Two alert types: `data_source_down` and `data_source_restored` (`notify.check_data_source_alerts`, module `account`) — inside the owner's daily cap and the hard ceiling (not in `NON_ALERT_TYPES`); `data_source_down` also carries the rating alert's "can't read your Google rating since …" fact.

**Restaurant DNA** (Benchmarking audit, workstream D, 9/24/26) — new. `GET /api/dna` + `/mobile/api/dna` (one body, `intelligence.dna.payload_for`; owner/manager logins, listed cross-module in `auth._UNGATED_PREFIXES`) return `{ok, profile}` for the login's OWN restaurant: `profile` = `{available, why_not?, week, as_of (M/D/YY), measured, of, coverage_pct, version, note, families: [{key, label, dimensions: [{key, code, label, value, display, unit, better, measured, basis, n, needs?, dormant, trend: {weeks, since_week, previous, previous_display, change, direction up|down|steady} | null, history: [{week (ISO week), value}]}]}]}` — `history` (10/7/26) is the restaurant's own stored weeks for a measured numeric dimension, oldest first, at most `dna.HISTORY_WEEKS` (12); `[]` for a categorical or unmeasured one. Web Home's Results and the iOS Restaurant DNA sheet chart it. A dimension of a module the login can't view is left out; an unmeasured one has `value: null` and `needs`. Never a z-score, another restaurant's figure or a personality label. `Cache-Control: no-store` on web. 400 with `error` when the login has no restaurant.

**Weekly review bands** (`weekly_review.build` → `metrics[]`, the digest's "trending" line): each row's `band` is `weekly_review.week_band` — max(stated band × `band_scale`, this restaurant's own 7-day band from `metrics.noise_band(window_days=7)`) — and the row adds `band_basis` (in words) and `false_alarm_rate` (null when the scaled stated band governed).

**Reviews read trend confidence**: `trend.confidence` (the rating-trend slope's band, what the web reads) and the top-level `confidence` (the same band, kept for shipped iOS builds) are both present on every path — fresh, stored, cached, stale fallback (`client_api._trend_confidence_both_places`). The "Do today" line's own K1 is `recs[].confidence_detail`, never either of these.

`GET /mobile/api/home/modules` (web twin `GET /api/home/modules`) → `{ok, modules}` — the Modules grid alone: the same `modules[]` tiles `/mobile/api/home` carries (`key, label, icon, status, kpi, pulse, mode` — see Food Cost, *Module tiles carry `mode`*), built by `mobile_api._home_module_tiles`. It builds no Home brief and records nothing (re-audit K5): the iOS Modules tab used to fetch all of `/mobile/api/home` for this list, and every open logged Home's cards as shown on a screen that renders none of them. Ask's opening fallback (`GET /mobile/api/ask-cavnar/opening` and its web twin, when the morning brief has no lines) builds Home with `home_brief.build_home_brief(..., present=False)` — answered keys still left out, nothing presented, nothing cached.

**Value — contract K4** (`value_delivered.headline` + `home_block`, one shape on both Homes): web `/api/home/brief` → `value`, and `/mobile/api/home` → `value` (beside the older flat `total_value_delivered`, `value_label`, `value_by_module`, `value_history`, kept for old clients) = `{total, net_monthly, worsened: {count, monthly, priced_count}, cumulative: <outcomes.cumulative>, unpriced_wins: [{title, module, line}], sales_lift: {monthly, net_monthly, wins, basis}, history, per: "month", label, by_module, caveat}`. `total` is the measured improvements per month; a surface shows `net_monthly` when `worsened.count > 0`. All of it is as THIS login may see it (`viewer_scope`); the day's `value_snapshots` point — what `history` draws — is the restaurant-wide **net** figure, written by the 6am `value_snapshots` job, never by a Home GET (10/7/26); a login that does not see everything gets `history: []`.

**Parity-round follow-ups (10/7/26) — additive, no new routes.** Push categories: `CAVNAR_COVERAGE` (Ask someone to cover · Resolved) for a `coverage` push, or an `issue` / `issue_escalated` push whose new `issue_kind` is `coverage` or `no_show`, carrying `issue_id`; `CAVNAR_ISSUE` is Resolved only; `critical_low` keeps `CAVNAR_STOCK` (`push._category`). `issues._push_instead` and `_fall_back` add `issue_kind` to the payload. The operator page (`platform_alert`, `ops.alert_will`) carries `nav: "admin/platform"`, `subject`, `lines` (≤ 6, each ≤ 200 chars) and `alert_at` (UTC ISO) — `ops.platform_alert_payload` — and reaches admin logins only, whoever is named (`push.ADMIN_ONLY_TYPES`); the app opens its "Platform needs you" sheet, with Open console → `https://dashboard.cavnar.ai/admin#operations`. A `schedule_drafted` push from a press adds `job_id` (the app keeps the banner down while that generation lands on screen). `GET /api/labor/shift-requests` (and `/mobile/api/…`) adds `can_decide` (SCHEDULE_DRAFT, the gate on decide / open-shift post / offer / cancel / colleague-agreed). Bell rows (`/api/notifications`, `/mobile/api/notifications`) with a drafted reply add `draft_complete` (false when `draft` is the first 600 characters) and `draft_hash` (`models.draft_hash`: sha256 hex of the stripped reply); `POST /approve/<id>` and `POST /mobile/api/reviews/<id>/approve` accept `expected_draft_hash` (and the web route now `expected_draft` too): a stored reply that no longer hashes to it answers 409 `draft_changed`, and a match is held to that exact text in the compare-and-set. The web bell sends it and approves in place only a whole reply; the app sends `expected_draft` (whole replies) and `expected_draft_hash`. `lineup_brief_waiting` is push-only (`strategy_jobs._reach(email=False)`).

**iOS parity round, Home / Ask / issues (10/7/26) — additive.** `GET /mobile/api/home?fresh=1` (the phone's pull-to-refresh) rebuilds the brief instead of serving `home_brief`'s 60-second cache, as `/api/home/brief?fresh=1` does; `home_brief.build_home_brief(reads=…)` takes the labor and inventory analyses `/mobile/api/home`'s tiles already ran, so a cold Home runs each once. Neither Home GET writes `value_snapshots`. `push.nav_for`: an `issue`, `issue_escalated` or `coverage` push carrying `issue_id` opens `issue/<id>` (coverage opened `labor/schedule`); the app's `CAVNAR_ISSUE` category runs `POST /mobile/api/issues/<id>/resolve` ("Resolved") or reads the issue and posts `ask-cover` with its next suggested cover ("Ask someone to cover") in the background. Restaurant DNA dimensions add `history: [{week, value}]` (below). The iOS confirm card sends `X-Cavnar-Proposal` / `X-Cavnar-Conversation` on the action request and polls `route.status.mobile` for a started job, as the web card does.

**What a card remembers — memory round (9/29/26), additive.** `/api/home/brief` and `/mobile/api/home` (whose `needs_attention[]` carries the same three answer fields):
- Each card (`recommendations[]`) and attention item carries `previous_answer` — `{answer, answered_on (M/D/YY), reason_code, reason_label, dollar_value, reopened, text}` or null: the card is back after the owner answered it (reopened on a material change — its figure doubled — re-armed when its trigger came back, or re-offered after its silence ran out), so it says "You passed on this on 3/12/26 ($120/mo then)" instead of asking as if for the first time; account-holder answers only — and `delegate_answer` — `{by, answer, reason_code, reason_label, answered_on, text}` or null, only in an account holder's payload: a manager passed on it, which held for that manager alone ("Dana passed on this: already doing it"); an admin's view-as answer is never named.
- `retest: true` on a card of a kind the owner had quieted, shown again because its re-test date came (`decisions.RETEST_AFTER_DAYS`, 60) or its figure doubled; `quieter[]` rows add `review_on` (M/D/YY), when a quieted kind gets its second chance.
- `conflict` on the weaker of two cards that pull against each other (`lever_conflicts`): `{id, rule: trim_vs_fill | reprice_vs_value | promote_vs_stock, label, with (the other card's title), why, choose: [{signature, key, label}], route: {web: "/api/recs/conflict", mobile: "/mobile/api/recs/conflict"}}`. The owner's choice goes to `POST recs/conflict` (see *Recommendation record*); a card they settled against is left out for a year. The same object rides on Marketing feed items, morning-brief lines and DSR actions, and the one-thing pick applies the same choices.
- The trim card (`trim_day:<Day>`) may carry `caution` (a service, wait or floor-staff complaint cluster on that weekday: the trim stands, says so, and ranks lower by its `rank_penalty`); a live campaign or post aimed at filling that night removes the card and adds a neutral `changes` line saying why (`staffing_signals.trim_guard`, the check every staffing cut makes). The labor change line reads "vs the week before".
- `kind_holds[]` — `{key: "kind_hold:<kind>", kind, title: "Keep suggesting <kind>?", why, measured, improved, worsened, do_nothing_pct, answers: {completed: "Keep suggesting it", not_for_us: "Stop suggesting it"}, rec_key, answerable: true}`: a kind this restaurant's own record says to stop proposing (no better than doing nothing, or worse, over its measured results — `rec_learning.Effectiveness.held`) is taken off the cards (never a critical one) and asked about instead, answered with the usual Done / Pass. `[]` when none.
- While the owner is fatigued (most of what they settled last month went dismissed or ignored — `learning_scorecard.volume_limit`), Home, the nightly report and the Marketing feed show fewer.

**Morning brief lines from last night's report and from memory** (`GET morning-brief` → `brief.lines[]`, memory round): `dsr_action` — the report's first unanswered priority under the report's own key (`rec`, `source: "dsr"`, `dsr_date`, `why`, `claim_kind: "inferred"`; Done / Pass answer it everywhere; Home's "Before service" card does not draw it, the Last night card has it); `today` with `source: "dsr"` — the report's forecast for today with its measured effects in the words (a game's own effect named without a figure when the game's item is among the items shown, its figure the game line's — `dsr.tomorrow.effect_words`; never an item about a later day), `confidence_pct` (how often its range has held), `predictions` ([text], the report's calls — "The report's calls"), `forecast`, `outside`, `dsr_date`; `memory:constraint` ("Your note for today: …", `claim_kind: "owner"`) and `memory:event` ("Remembered: …", `claim_kind: "measured"` — behind the Labor view, like the forecast's effects, and never for the game the `event_ahead` line speaks for), at most two memory lines, only for something dated today.

**The game line** (`brief.lines[]` key `event_ahead:<event_id>`, `event_intel.playbook.alert`, Event Intelligence 10/1/26): the next followed headline game within three days — `{key, event_id, source: "events", others, tone, claim_kind, outside, text, ask, staffing?, prep?, action?}`. `others` are the other headline games' ids that date (the one with a measured effect leads); `claim_kind` is `inferred` only when the text says a staffing plan (then `tone: "action"` and `staffing: {recommend, basis, n}` are present), `computed` when it says other restaurants' nights, else `measured` or `context`; `prep` past the item-mix floor (behind `gameday.item_mix_visible`); `action` (Marketing) opens Campaign Studio with the goal and send time; the text time is said only to a reader `gameday.guest_text_visible` allows (the Marketing module and the Marketing view) and never once it has passed (`gameday.plan_ahead`); `ask` is the game's own question (`gameday.ask_for`). The game's effect is said here once, with `engine.effect_for`'s figure, and not again for a part already said for this game (`playbook._said`); the staffing sentence says the plan and what was staffed, never a lift of its own. Every time is on the restaurant's clock.

**What connects remembers** (`GET cross-module`, both prefixes): each link carries `memory: {first_seen, last_seen, times_seen, weeks_running, recurring, came_back: {on, after, resolved_on} | null, declined, resolved, label}` — links are kept (`link_memory`), not recomputed as new each read; `recurring` at 3 weeks running (`link_memory.ESCALATE_WEEKS`) or when it came back after it went away or was answered. Show `memory.label` under the headline ("Found 3 weeks running, since 9/7/26", "Still found after you marked it done on 9/14/26"). The one-thing candidate from a link is the longest-standing one, and `fix_first` may carry `recurring` and `link_memory`. Two new kinds, `dsr_x_reviews` and `marketing_x_labor`, carry the usual fields with `act.nav` `labor/schedule?day=<Day>`; clients must tolerate kinds they do not know. Re-audit (9/29/26): `links` holds only links the owner — or this login — has not answered (`business_intelligence.unanswered_links`; a Done link found again 28+ days later comes back with `memory.came_back`); a `reviews_x_labor` link may carry `covers` `{day, nights, gplh, other_nights, other_gplh}` (guests per labor hour the nightly reports measured); a `reviews_x_menu` link carries `menu_item_id` / `menu_item`; `fix_first` may carry `agrees_with` `{signature, on (M/D/YY), title, text}` (the owner declined the opposite staffing advice — this agrees), and the whole-schedule candidate `start_day` (or null) and `trim_guards` `[{day, why}]` (heavier days a campaign is filling on the next draft). Ask's `read_business_snapshot` adds `links_answered` (a count) and `read_restaurant_memory` adds `links` — every kept link with `status` ("open", "you said not for us", "you marked it done", "the change was made", "no longer found", "its night was measured", "its night passed with nothing measured"), `resolved_on`, `first_found`, `last_found` (M/D/YY), `label`, `times_came_back`, projected by the login's module views. The morning brief's `money` line text ends with the labor line's `guard` when a campaign fills the day a trim would start from.

**Data the owner said they don't trust** (`GET /api/data-health`, `/mobile/api/data-health`): `distrusted: [{source, label, since (M/D/YY), reports, text, verify: {web, mobile, source}}]` — a "don't trust the data" answer on a card holds every card resting on that source at low evidence until it is re-verified. `POST data-health/verify` `{source}` (a `strategy_routes._ROUTES` entry; account holder only) → `{ok, released, message: "Thanks — recommendations resting on it are back at full confidence"}` — lifts the cap and releases the answers held for it; 400 without a source, 404 when nothing waits there.

**The K1 Why panel's prior** — `dimensions.accuracy` adds `prior_rung` (the named group a cohort figure came from) and `prior_unlock: "confirm_profile"` when confirming the restaurant profile would unlock a finer group ("Confirm your restaurant profile to compare with restaurants like yours", linking to Account → Restaurant profile).

### The Command Center and nav paths (`command_center.py` via `strategy_routes._ROUTES`, each at `/api/…` and `/mobile/api/…`; Friction audit 9/25/26)
No model call behind any of these; listed cross-module in `auth._UNGATED_PREFIXES` (`/api/command/`), projected by the login's permissions inside.
- `GET /command/registry` → `{ok, commands:[{id, label, keywords:[str], kind: "nav"|"action"|"ask", tier: 0|1|2, nav: str|null, action: str|null, args: {}|null}]}` — the places (modules, sections, `account/<section>`, the daily report, other locations as `location/<id>`), the confirmable actions (Ask's write tools `approve_all_reviews`, `publish_schedule`, `send_supplier_order`, `generate_schedule`, `refresh_competitors` — each only when `ask_cavnar_tools.tool_allowed` on the login's `viewer_restaurant` AND the route's own permission, e.g. `SCHEDULE_PUBLISH`, allow it) and `ask`. `tier` is the confirm/undo policy's (DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §10): 2 goes outside the restaurant, 1 changes data here, 0 navigates. A place for a module the restaurant doesn't have or the login can't read is not listed; Billing and People only for an account holder.
- `GET /command/search?q=` → `{ok, results:[{type, id, title, subtitle, nav, location:{id, name}}]}` — `type` ∈ place, location, review, person, issue, schedule, dsr, chat; at most 20, ranked (a field starting with the query, then a word, then a substring). Under 2 characters → `[]`. Reviews need the Reviews view, people and schedules Labor; issues respect `LOSS_VIEW`; chats are the caller's own. Every result names its location; only a `location` result is another one. People come from `people.list_people` when it exists, else the roster names.
- `POST /command/propose` `{action, args}` → `{ok, proposal}` — the SAME proposal object Ask renders (`build_proposal`: `summary`, `route`, `body`, `fields_shown`, `details`, `preview`, `at_stake`) plus `proposal_id` (its `ask_cavnar_actions` "proposed" row, `surface='command'` — never a Still-open item), `target` (the path ids its route carries), `tier` and `surface: "command"`. `decide_time_off` / `decide_shift_request` need SCHEDULE_DRAFT, as the decide routes do. **A confirm settles itself (re-audit F1-9):** the web card posts its action route with the header `X-Cavnar-Proposal: <proposal_id>` (and `X-Cavnar-Conversation`); when that route answers 200 `ok` (not a started `job_id`, not `needs_ack`) and is that proposal's route, `command_center.settle_confirmed` records the confirm in the same request, scoped like `/api/ask-cavnar/action`, and adds `proposal_settled: true` — the client then sends no second request. `PROPOSAL_DENYLIST` and the own-domain link refusal apply to `args` as to a model's (400 with the reason; nothing logged). A read tool → 400; an action the login may not run → 403. Confirm and dismiss go through `POST /api/ask-cavnar/action` with the `proposal_id`, like any Ask card. Rate-limited (30 a minute per restaurant → 429).
- `GET /ask-cavnar/proposals/<id>` → `{ok, proposal}` — a stored, unanswered Ask proposal rebuilt from its row by `build_proposal` (an order's draft hash re-read from the order as it stands; a per-review id from the stored summary), keeping its `proposal_id`, `surface: "reopened"`. Settled → `{ok, proposal: null, settled: {outcome, at}, summary}`. Another restaurant's id → 404; another login's → 404 unless the caller is an account holder (the Ask action route's rule); an action the login can no longer run → 403.

**`nav` on every "take me there" payload** (nav.py — `<head>[/<rest>][?query]`, opened by web `cavNav` and iOS `NavPath`): Home `attention[].action.nav` (`home_brief.ATTENTION_NAV`: "Reply now" → `reviews?filter=urgent`, "See the list" → `inventory/order`, else the module) and `quick_actions[].nav` (`QUICK_NAV`); `/api/actions` `items[].nav` and `items[].action.nav` (an outward action — Send now, a request's Approve / Deny — also carries `action.confirm: {action, args}`, the command action the web opens as a confirm card via `/command/propose` instead of posting the route; the route and `confirm` are only on items the login holds the route's permission for — SCHEDULE_PUBLISH, SCHEDULE_DRAFT — else the action is "Open it"; a stored Ask proposal is listed only to its own login or an account holder, only while `command_center.action_allowed`, and never a `surface='command'` one — re-audit F1-3, F1-7, F2-16) (`action_queue.nav_for`: `issue/<id>`, `action/<proposal id>` — a stored proposal reopens without a model call —, `request/time_off-<id>` / `request/shift_request-<id>`, `schedule/<history id>`, `labor/schedule`, `inventory/invoices`, `reviews?filter=pending`); `/api/notifications` (and the mobile twin) `notifications[].nav` (`nav.for_notification`: a review alert → `review/<id>`, a brief → `ask?q=<its question>`, a big-game heads-up (`event_ahead`, ref `catalog_event`) → `ask?q=` that game's own question, the one its push carried (`gameday.ask_for`, on the location's clock; the type's "How should we get ready for our next game?" once the game is gone — listed and badged only for logins with the Labor view, the push's own audience, though its `module` stays `ask`: `client_api.sees_alert` → `push.audience_of`, the rule Home's alert list uses too), a held schedule → `labor/schedule`, a sign-in → `account/security`, an issue → `issue`). An older client ignores it.

The web page reads `?nav=<path>&loc=<restaurant id>` (every alert email button, `notify.alert_url`: `nav.for_notification`'s path — never an `ask` one, which stays its module — and the alert's location; re-audit F1-13) and the older `?review=<id>` / `?tab=<module>`, switches to `loc` first when the login can (landing on the item after the reload), opens the item, then drops every one of these parameters; the address after the `#` is a nav path too (`#labor/schedule`, `#account/notifications`, `#reviews?filter=urgent`, `#dsr/2026-09-24`, `#recs`), restored on reload and walked by Back.

### The owner's day (`strategy_routes.py`, web + mobile twins)
`actions` (everything still open, filtered by what the reader may see; labor items act in place since Friction #4/#18 — a time-off or shift request's `action` is Approve on its decide route with `alt` Deny and `nav` `request/time_off-<id>` / `request/shift_request-<id>`; an unsent week's "Send now" posts `labor/publish-schedule` `{schedule_id}` (a `409 needs_ack` is answered in place) with `nav` `schedule/<id>` and `alt` "Open it"; `module` stays on each for older clients), `actions/snooze` `{key, days}`, `account/targets` (GET `{targets:{labor_target_pct, food_cost_target, waste_target_pct, monthly_revenue_target, weekly_revenue_target, hourly_rate, week_start_day, role_rates, roles, sched_notes, salaried, salaried_names, sources}, can_edit, sees_pay}` — `role_rates`/`hourly_rate` only for the owner and `SCHEDULE_PUBLISH` holders, empty/null otherwise (F2-20); `salaried` (`[{name, annual, per_day}]`) and `salaried_names` (the roster's names, for the add box) only for the account holder, `[]` otherwise; POST any subset, owner only (`TEAM_INVITE`), bounds-checked, `weekly_revenue_target` the same one revenue target by the week (stored as the monthly × 52/12; sent with `monthly_revenue_target`, the weekly wins; 9/28/26), `role_rates` a `{role: $/h}` map, `salaried_add` `{name, annual}` ($1,000–$2,000,000; a name already listed is replaced) and `salaried_remove` `name` edit `salaried_staff_json` one person per save on the server (9/28/26), `sched_notes` the owner's standing notes every schedule draft's prompt carries (plain text, control characters dropped, 2,000 characters; empty clears — the schedule workspace's Advanced AI panel, 9/26/26), and a target it carries is marked `…_source: set` even when it equals the seeded value — Friction #26; admin settings stay an override), `closeout` (GET tonight's + the questions: `fields`, `dsr_fields`, `labels`, and `suggested` `{callouts, influence, sources}` — tonight's open no-show issues and dated events/holiday as editable prefill, never saved on their own (Friction #40); POST any of the ten fields — the four quick lines and `equipment`, `vip_guests`, `maintenance`, `shift_notes`, `general_notes`, `influence`; a field left out keeps what was filed, an empty string clears it). `morning-brief` now returns the caller's own brief plus `can_edit`; `morning-brief/settings` also takes `hold_alerts` and `preshift_nudge_hour`. `GET morning-brief?view=home` (K6) is the read Home's "Before service" card makes: the lines it renders — every keyed line but `fix_first` and `money`, which Home's focus card shows — are presented on `home` for this login, once per key a day. Without `view=home` (the settings screens) nothing is recorded.

### The nightly DSR (`strategy_routes.py`, each at `/api/…` and `/mobile/api/…`)
Any console login; employee and support logins get 403. Every payload carries `view` — `owner` for an owner login at the location (owner / client role, or a Cavnar admin), `manager` for every other console login — decided by `dsr.access.view_for`. Both views render from the same stored facts; the manager view drops blocks the role can't read (Food without FOOD_COST_VIEW, …), loss lines (comps / voids / refunds) — and `gross` / `gross_items`, which beside net and discounts ARE the comps (and on the "all" basis the voids) — without LOSS_VIEW, and owner-only financials (`budget*`, `vs_budget*`, `prime_cost*`, `source_checks`), the narrative items that cite them or name their subject in words (budget, prime cost, food cost, comps/voids/refunds), and the predictions resting on them (`sales_budget`). The raw `facts.tomorrow` is never in any payload — only the per-view `tomorrow`. Always the session's own restaurant.
- `GET dsr?limit=&before=` — recent nights, latest version each: `{business_date, label (M/D/YY), version, status, provisional, missing, finalized_at, net, lead, lead_missing}` — `net` only when the Sales block is ready (never 0 for unknown), `lead` the same opening the report view leads with for this login (`access.narrative_for` — a manager's is the operations summary), `lead_missing` why there is none ("The summary rests on figures outside your view" when a summary exists but none of it is this view's). The list also carries `enabled` (`dsr_enabled`) and `tonight` (the business date Close day would run). Home's "Last night" card reads `limit=1`. Each row also carries Home's status contract (density fix #1, 9/25/26), all None-safe: `verdict` ("Good day"), `tone` (good|warn|bad), `overall` (0–100) — the owner's scorecard as the report builds it (`dsr.scorecard`, live budget applied), null for a manager, whose report has no score; `vs_budget` (net minus budget, dollars) only in a view that may read the budget; `first_risk` — the owner's first scorecard risk that is not the budget line, a manager's first needs-attention line that survived the cite filter.
- `GET dsr/<YYYY-MM-DD>?version=` — the night: `facts` (blocks, `missing`, `withheld`, `fiscal`), `narrative`, `scorecard` (owner view only, else null — `dsr.scorecard.build`: `{overall: 0–100|null, verdict: {label: Excellent day|Good day|Mixed day|Tough day, tone}|null, components: [{key: sales|labor|food|guests, label, measured, value, detail, tone, why}], wins: [{text, key, source: measured|narrative}], risks: [...], basis}`; the email's subject/preheader and the push lead with it), `kpis` (both views — `dsr.kpis`: `[{key, label, value, value_text, unit, better, change: {delta, text, tone}|null, streak: {text, tone}|null, spark: [..], target: {value_text, label}|null, peers: {available, label, value_text, why_not, strength_pct}|null, derived, estimate, basis?}]` — `prime_pct` is (labor $ + estimated recipe cost) ÷ net, withheld under the recipe-coverage floor, with its `basis`; a manager's `kpis` and `operations` never share a key), `operations` and `shift` (manager only: `shift: {rows: [{key, label, value_text, tone}], note}` — `no_shows` is labelled "No-shows"), `insights` (`[{kind, label, text, source}]`), `tomorrow` (`dsr.tomorrow`: `{date, weekday, items: [{kind, tone, text}], scheduled, forecast: {text, basis}|null, confidence: {pct|null, label ("62%" or "—"), based_on, missing: [], watch: [..], track}|null, predictions}` — `confidence.pct` is the forecast's measured record (how often its stated range held, out of sample, `demand.demand_accuracy` inside_range_pct over ≥ 10 ranged nights) and null below that or with no range; the weather, schedule and events are `watch`, never confidence; `predictions` without the ones the view may not see), `yesterday` (`dsr.predictions.review`: `{items: [{key, text, outcome: correct|incorrect|null, actual_text}], accuracy: {pct|null, correct, graded, window_days, min_graded}}` | null — a prediction written after its night opened is void and never listed; the view's hidden kinds are left out and not counted), `checklist`, `versions` (each with `finalized_at_local` / `created_at_local`, the restaurant's wall clock), `status`, `provisional`, `trigger`. 404 when there is none; 400 on a bad date. The web screen is `/#dsr/YYYY-MM-DD` — the link every DSR email's "View full report" carries.
  The narrative's single slots (AI cost audit 10/7/26 #78): a night written since the merge carries `biggest_risk`, `biggest_win`, `biggest_staffing_concern`, `highest_priority_issue`, `largest_opportunity`, `largest_guest_experience` (and the retired `largest_money_saving`, always null); a stored night from before it may also carry `biggest_financial_opportunity` and `largest_staffing`, which clients keep rendering.
- `GET dsr/<YYYY-MM-DD>/status` — the progressive checklist: `stages` [{key, label, at, at_local, done, current}], `blocks` [{name, label, status, reason, at, at_local}], `narrative`, `closed_by`, `next_attempt_at`, `missing`, `rerun` (`{at, refused, why}` when a re-run was refused because the POS didn't return the night's sales — the report is unchanged — else null); `exists: false` before the night has started.
- `POST dsr/close` `{date?, rerun?, early?}` — Close day: starts the night now (trigger `manual`) on a background thread; 202 `{started: true}`, 200 `{started: false, status: "final"}` when it's already final. It never overrules a POS that keeps a close-day record (RPOWER): the night waits (`awaiting_close`) for the POS's own close. On a POS without one, before its close time less 30 minutes it is 409 `{code: "before_close", needs_confirm: <owner>, error}` — an owner confirms a genuine early close with `early: true`. `rerun: true` re-runs a finished night as a new version — owner view only (403 otherwise, checked before the date); the sales are pulled first and, when the POS doesn't return them, no version is made (the status's `rerun` says why). `date` defaults to tonight's business date; a manager may close only tonight or the night before, an owner any of the last 7 business dates (`DSR_OWNER_DAYS`) — the "generate one if the automation failed" path (400 outside the window); 409 when `dsr_enabled` is off; rate-limited per restaurant.
- `GET dsr/week?date=` / `GET dsr/period?date=` — Erik's weekly grid and the fiscal period (`dsr.rollup` through `access.redact_grid`: budget columns for the owner view only; labor columns only with LABOR_VIEW; gross only with LOSS_VIEW; `withheld` lists them, and the .xlsx leaves those columns out). Last Year is `store.last_year_day` (the same fiscal week and weekday of the previous fiscal year where a calendar is kept, else 364 days back) read through `store.baselines_net` — the nightly report's own resolver: its DSR, an imported workbook row, or the POS sync's daily total only where that total is the DSR's basis (RPOWER). Each day row carries `gross_basis` (`items` | `all` — the basis that night was built under, read from the night itself; null with no report). Every totals block (week, period to date, each week of a period, the period) carries `days_measured` (nights with a net), `<column>_days` per summed column — `gross_days` can be fewer than `days_measured` when an everything-rung night had no tax or voids from the POS — `gross_bases` (the sorted set of bases the gross total adds) and `gross_mixed` (true when that set has two). A period's length is its own year's (a listed 53-week year's five-week period is five weeks).
Any console login; employee and support logins get 403. Every payload carries `view` — `owner` for an owner login at the location (owner / client role, or a Cavnar admin), `manager` for every other console login — decided by `dsr.access.view_for`. Both views render from the same stored facts; the manager view drops blocks the role can't read (Food without FOOD_COST_VIEW, …), loss lines (comps / voids / refunds) without LOSS_VIEW, and owner-only financials (`budget*`, `vs_budget*`, `prime_cost*`, `source_checks`), and the narrative items that cite them. Always the session's own restaurant.
- `GET dsr?limit=&before=` — recent nights, latest version each: `{business_date, label (M/D/YY), version, status, provisional, missing, finalized_at, net, lead, lead_missing}` — `net` only when the Sales block is ready (never 0 for unknown), `lead` the summary's opening sentence as this login may read it, `lead_missing` why there is none. The list also carries `enabled` (`dsr_enabled`) and `tonight` (the business date Close day would run). Home's "Last night" card reads `limit=1`. Each row also carries Home's status contract (density fix #1, 9/25/26), all None-safe: `verdict` ("Good day"), `tone` (good|warn|bad), `overall` (0–100) — the owner's scorecard as the report builds it (`dsr.scorecard`, live budget applied), null for a manager, whose report has no score; `vs_budget` (net minus budget, dollars) only in a view that may read the budget; `first_risk` — the owner's first scorecard risk that is not the budget line, a manager's first needs-attention line that survived the cite filter.
- `GET dsr/<YYYY-MM-DD>?version=` — the night: `facts` (blocks, `missing`, `withheld`, `fiscal`), `narrative`, `scorecard` (owner view only, else null — `dsr.scorecard.build`: `{overall: 0–100|null, verdict: {label: Excellent day|Good day|Mixed day|Tough day, tone}|null, components: [{key: sales|labor|food|guests, label, measured, value, detail, tone, why}], wins: [{text, key, source: measured|narrative}], risks: [...], basis}`; the email's subject/preheader and the push lead with it), `kpis` (both views — `dsr.kpis`: `[{key, label, value, value_text, unit, better, change: {delta, text, tone}|null, streak: {text, tone}|null, spark: [..], target: {value_text, label}|null, peers: {available, label, value_text, why_not, strength_pct}|null, derived, estimate}]`), `operations` and `shift` (manager only: `shift: {rows: [{key, label, value_text, tone}], note}`), `insights` (`[{kind, label, text, source}]`), `tomorrow` (`dsr.tomorrow`: `{date, weekday, items: [{kind, tone, text}], scheduled, forecast: {text, basis}|null, confidence: {pct, based_on, missing, track}|null, predictions}`), `yesterday` (`dsr.predictions.review`: `{items: [{key, text, outcome: correct|incorrect|null, actual_text}], accuracy: {pct|null, correct, graded, window_days, min_graded}}` | null), `checklist`, `versions` (each with `finalized_at_local` / `created_at_local`, the restaurant's wall clock), `status`, `provisional`, `trigger`. 404 when there is none; 400 on a bad date. The web screen is `/#dsr/YYYY-MM-DD`; every DSR email's "View full report" carries `/?rid=<restaurant>#dsr/YYYY-MM-DD` (and its "Ask about this" links carry `rid=` too): a login whose session sits on another location is switched to the report's own through `/api/switch-location` (which decides whether it may), `rid` is dropped from the address, and the night opens after the reload — a refused switch opens the night on the current location.
- `GET dsr/<YYYY-MM-DD>/status` — the progressive checklist: `stages` [{key, label, at, at_local, done, current}], `blocks` [{name, label, status, reason, at, at_local}], `narrative`, `closed_by`, `next_attempt_at`, `missing`; `exists: false` before the night has started.
- `POST dsr/close` `{date?, rerun?}` — Close day: starts the night now (trigger `manual`) on a background thread; 202 `{started: true}`, 200 `{started: false, status: "final"}` when it's already final. `rerun: true` re-runs a finished night as a new version — owner view only (403 otherwise, checked before the date). `date` defaults to tonight's business date; a manager may close only tonight or the night before, an owner any of the last 7 business dates (`DSR_OWNER_DAYS`) — the "generate one if the automation failed" path (400 outside the window); 409 when `dsr_enabled` is off; rate-limited per restaurant.
- `GET dsr/week?date=` / `GET dsr/period?date=` — Erik's weekly grid and the fiscal period (`dsr.rollup` through `access.redact_grid`: budget columns for the owner view only; labor columns only with LABOR_VIEW). Each day row carries `gross_basis` (`items` | `all` — the basis that night was built under, read from the night itself; null with no report). Every totals block (week, period to date, each week of a period, the period) carries `days_measured` (nights with a net), `<column>_days` per summed column — `gross_days` can be fewer than `days_measured` when an everything-rung night had no tax or voids from the POS — `gross_bases` (the sorted set of bases the gross total adds) and `gross_mixed` (true when that set has two). A period's length is its own year's (a listed 53-week year's five-week period is five weeks).
- Density round (9/25/26), additive: the night adds `kpis_headline` (the keys of the few `kpis` the report leads with — `dsr.kpis.headline`: at most 4, and for the owner never one of Today's score's measured components; every KPI stays in `kpis`) and `shift.verdict` (`{text, tone}` | null — "Labor 27.5%, on target · 1 no-show", from the Labor block's own figures); `operations` holds at most `kpis.OPERATIONS_MAX` (4); `insights` holds at most 2 and never `biggest_win` / `biggest_risk` / `highest_priority_issue` or the old `at_stake` kind. `dsr/week` and `dsr/period` add `story` (string | null — `dsr.rollup.story` via `access.grid_for`, written from the view's redacted grid, so a manager's never names the budget). The DSR email presents only the three actions it prints to `rec_ledger` (`surface: dsr_email`).
- KPI round 1 (9/30/26), additive: the night adds `kpis_big` (the six lead tiles, `dsr.kpis.big`: `[{key, label, value, value_text, unit, sub: [≤2 lines], tone, trend: {dir: up|down|flat, text, vs, tone}|null, rank: hero|secondary|tertiary, spark?, bar?: {value, max, marker, label}, gauge?: {value, target, soft}, note?, estimate?}]` — the hero first; 10/1/26) and `pace` (`{week, period}` from `dsr.kpis.pace`: `net`, `nights_measured`, `nights_total`, `nights_left`, `last_year`, `last_year_pct`, and for the owner only `budget_to_date`, `budget_vs`, `budget_vs_pct`, `budget_total`, `budget_left`, `budget_per_night_needed`, `budget_label`; `typical_left` / `typical_per_night` from the forecast). `tomorrow` adds `labor` (`hours`, `hourly_cost`, `hourly_pct`, `overtime_hours`, `forecast_net`, `target_pct`, `people`, `basis`; owner only `salaried_cost`, `salaried_total_cost`, `salaried_total_pct`) and `overtime` (`people: [{employee, role, projected_hours, overtime_hours, extra_cost, room}]`, `over_count`, `extra_cost`, `basis`) — both dropped without LABOR_VIEW, with `rest_day` items. `facts.blocks` adds `service` (`dsr.block_service`); `facts.blocks.labor.detail` adds `departments` and `hourly_hours`; `facts.blocks.sales.detail` adds `hourly_typical`.
- `GET dsr/week.xlsx?date=` — the week as a real .xlsx (`dsr.xlsx.week_workbook`) in the grid's layout: title row "Simple EJ's · Period 9 · Week 4 · 9/16/26 – 9/22/26", header, a row per day, week total, period to date; `$#,##0` and `0.0%` formats, frozen header; built from the same redacted payload, so a manager's file has no budget. `Content-Disposition: attachment`.
- `POST dsr/budget` `{date, gross, net}` or `{days: [{date, gross, net}, …]}` (≤14) — owner only (403 for every other login). A blank figure clears it; negative or non-numeric is 400.
- `POST dsr/category` `{pos_name, category}` — owner only. Map a POS department to one of the six (matched case-insensitively) or the owner's own label; "Unmapped" is refused. Counts from the next report on.
- `GET dsr/settings` / `POST dsr/settings` — owner only. GET: `settings` {`fiscal_week_start_dow` (0=Mon…6=Sun), `fiscal_year_start`, `fiscal_period_scheme` (`4x13` | `445` | `454` | `544` | the exact period lengths in weeks, comma-separated: 12–13 of 4 or 5 totalling 52 or 53), `dsr_enabled`, `dsr_notify` (email + push the finished report; off by default), `dsr_gross_basis` (`items` | `all`: gross as items only, or everything rung incl. tax and voids), `dsr_deadline_hour`, `calendar_label`, `fiscal_years` — the years that run differently, `[{start, start_label (M/D/YY), lengths, weeks, fiscal_year}]`, `[]` when none}, `categories`, `category_map`, `held` ({pos_name: [POS item categories]} recorded for each mapped department) and `contents` (what the latest report saw inside each department), and `unmapped` — the departments the latest report couldn't place, with their dollars (`unmapped_as_of`); one new inside a mapped department carries `new_in` (the department) and `mapped_to` (where that department counts), named "Department › Category" — `POST dsr/category` with that name places it. POST: a JSON object with any of the eight settings — `fiscal_period_scheme` also as a list of ints, `fiscal_years` as `[{start: "YYYY-MM-DD", lengths: [ints] | "445"…}]` (`[]` or null clears it); Period 1 and every listed year must start on the week's first day, listed years must not overlap (400 otherwise, the reason in M/D/YY). A value of the wrong type — a list or object for `dsr_gross_basis`, a bool for `dsr_deadline_hour`, a body that is not an object — is a 400 with the reason, never a 500.
- `POST dsr/history/import` — **owner view only** (403 otherwise). Last Year from the owner's old DSR workbooks: multipart field `file`, `.xlsx` or `.csv`, at most 2 MB (the app's 5 MB cap answers 413 above that). Reads the template layout today (`dsr.history_import`; header row `Date, Gross, Net, Food, Liquor, Beer, Wine, Retail, NA Beverage`, matched loosely — case, spacing, "Day", "NA Bev", "Net Sales"; every sheet with that header; Excel serial or M/D/YY / ISO dates; `$1,234.50` and `(12.00)` figures). A blank cell is never a zero: a day with neither gross nor net is `skipped`; a bad figure, bad or future date, or a repeated date is an entry in `errors` ("P9 W1 row 6: Gross '$abc' isn't a number.") and that row alone is not imported. 200 `{ok: true, imported, skipped, errors: [...]}` (a re-import of a day replaces it; `dsr.rollup`'s Last Year reads it); 400 `{ok: false, error, imported: 0, skipped: 0, errors: [error]}` when the file can't be read at all (wrong type, damaged, no matching header, empty, too large); rate-limited per restaurant (429). Always the session's restaurant. Erik's own weekly-grid workbook layout is not read yet — it is added as a second layout when his file arrives.
- `GET dsr/history/template.csv` — the import template, header row only (`text/csv`, attachment `dsr-last-year-template.csv`); any login with a DSR view.
- Memory round (9/29/26), additive:
  - **The forecast on the report's own basis.** The sales block's forecast baseline (`facts.blocks.sales.detail.baselines.forecast`) is `demand.forecast_net`: `{net, source: "demand.forecast_net", samples, basis (words, naming each measured effect), forecast_basis, same_basis: true, base_net (the plain weekday median), effect_pct, effects: [{display, label, lift_pct, n, …}], effect_basis, basis_note}`, or `{net: null, source: null, reason}` when there is none (a POS whose daily total is counted another way waits for enough of the report's own nights). Each night stores `sales.forecast_same_basis`, so forecast accuracy scores only same-basis nights (*Forecasts*, below).
  - **The owner's goal as the target.** `sales.detail.budget` is `{gross, net, source: budget | goal, label, goal_id}`: with no budget for the night the owner's nightly-sales goal stands in, said as a goal ("Your goal of $9,000 a night by 12/1/26"), never as a budget they did not enter (`dsr.store.night_budget`). The `prime_pct` KPI's `target` is the owner's prime-cost goal — `{value, value_text, label: "Your goal by 12/1/26", source: "goal", goal_id}` — or null: Cavnar AI keeps no prime-cost target of its own.
  - `tomorrow.forecast` adds `base`, `effect_pct` and `effects`, and its `basis` names each measured effect ("…, Trivia night +12% (measured 6 times here)").
  - Event re-audit 2 (10/1/26), one figure per game: a `tomorrow.forecast.effects[]` entry for a game whose `event` item states its effect carries `game_event_id`, and the basis names it without a figure ("Bears home game · Soldier Field's measured effect" — `dsr.tomorrow.effect_words`; the web chip shows the name only), since the item says it with `engine.effect_for`'s figure. `items[]` of kind `event` add `plain` (the game without its lift — what a login without the Labor view reads in the brief), `has_effect` and `event_id`; `game_staffing` adds `plan` (a staffing plan, not only what was staffed) and its `text` states no lift. `event_ahead` items (a game two or three days out) stay in the report and are never carried into the brief's today line. A snapshot stored before this reads as it did.
  - `facts.blocks.intel.detail.weather.observed` — `{high_f, low_f, precip_in, rained_in_service, conditions, station, summary, basis}` — once the nearest National Weather Service station's observation of that night is on file (the 5am `event_memory` job; added at read time, `dsr.access`); the stored forecast stays labelled a forecast. Show it as "Weather (actual)" beside the forecast.
  - `narrative.actions_tomorrow[]` may carry `caution` and `rank_penalty` (a staffing cut on a night with a service-complaint cluster: kept, said, ranked lower) and `conflict` (as on Home). `narrative.verification.dropped[].why` gains the staffing guard's reason (a campaign aimed at filling that night) and "the owner chose the other advice when these conflicted"; while the owner is fatigued only the top of the ranking is kept.

### Strategic foundations (`strategy_routes.py`, each at `/api/…` and `/mobile/api/…`)
`issues` (GET list / POST create), `issues/<id>/resolve`, `issues/<id>/reassign`, `issues/<id>/ask-cover` (all three 404 on a `kind='loss'` issue for a login without LOSS_VIEW, or an issue tagged with a module the login may not open — a Monday-plan item on food cost for a login without Food Cost — as the list leaves them out: `strategy_routes._issue_hidden` → `issues.viewer_sees_issue`, re-audit PEOPLE-14) (POST `{name}` — LABOR_VIEW; texts, or emails, one of the coverage issue's suggested covers the request; SMS only to a consented number), `issues/routing` (GET/POST, principal-only), `issues/routing/contact` (POST `{role, name, phone, consent}`, principal-only — adds a consented alert contact, or grants consent to the one already on that number, and routes `role` to them; 400 without consent, or past `notify.MAX_ALERT_CONTACTS` (2) people; 9/27/26), `goals` (GET/POST), `goals/<id>/end` (404 for a goal whose metric the login may not see — `_metric_visible`; 403 for anyone but an account holder — a manager's goal is only a proposal, and support through view-as is not the owner; memory re-audit 9/29/26, PEOPLE-6), `outcomes` (GET/POST), `outcomes/<id>/abandon`, `metrics`, `food-cost/dish-scorecard`, `food-cost/reprice` (each suggestion carries `rec_key` `reprice:<dish>`; answered dishes are left out), `food-cost/reprice/apply` (POST `{dish, price?}` — one tap to the suggested price, or the owner's adjusted one; records suggested vs chosen, answers the recommendation and starts the outcome tracker; 409 when the dish has no live suggestion — web `/api` and `/mobile/api` twins in client_api / mobile_api), `food-cost/invoices` (GET list / POST multipart `file`), `food-cost/invoices/<id>`, `food-cost/invoices/<id>/apply`, `food-cost/recipe-drafts` (GET — drafts plus `missing` and `ingredients` counts), `food-cost/recipe-drafts/<id>/accept|reject`, `food-cost/recipes/draft` (POST `{menu}` — the pasted menu, one dish a line with an optional price; empty body drafts the POS dishes with no recipe; at most `RECIPE_DRAFT_LIMIT` model calls a request, `remaining` says how many are left), `food-cost/recipes/import` (POST CSV), `food-cost/recipes/scan` (POST multipart photo — iOS only now), `labor/demand?day=`, `labor/auto-draft` (GET/POST; `enabled` read as a boolean, "false" is off; logged `auto_draft_changed`; GET adds `weekday` (0 = Monday), `day` and `publish_day`, POST takes `weekday` 0–5 — 400 for Sunday, a bool or a word — logged `auto_draft_day_changed`), `labor/auto-publish` GET adds `day` (the day after the draft day) and `draft_day`, `food-cost/auto-order` POST (owner only since F2-14 — a standing supplier-spend rule; GET adds `weekday` and `day`, POST takes `enabled` and/or `weekday` 0–6, logged `auto_order_day_changed`), `actions/pending` (a queued supplier order's `label` loses its dollar total for a login without FOOD_COST_VIEW — `delayed.for_viewer`; the activity feed's `queued` likewise), `actions/<id>/cancel` (the power to make the send: `SCHEDULE_PUBLISH` for `schedule_publish`/`schedule_changes_send`, FOOD_COST_VIEW for `order_send`, else the owner — 403 otherwise; F3-13), `people/<key>` (GET adds `editable {role: false, pay_rate: false, job_title}` and `pay_rate_amount` — the `pay_rate {role, rate, source}` object's rate as a number; `pay_rate` only for the owner and `SCHEDULE_PUBLISH` holders; 409 when a key names two people. POST refuses a CHANGED `role` or `pay_rate` with a 400 sentence — the same value echoed back is ignored; every refusal comes before any write — F3-5/F2-13; two names that slug alike get distinct keys `slug-xxxx`), `food-cost/count-sheet` (GET adds `ledger_mark`; POST with it answers `409 {needs_confirm, deliveries[]}` when a delivery was posted after the sheet opened, until `deliveries: counted|after` — `after` adds what arrived to the count; F2-7), `loss-signals` (LOSS_VIEW: owners, or a manager granted it), `morning-brief` (the caller's own brief) + `morning-brief/settings` (principal-only). Notifications: `GET notifications` (scoped to what the login may see; marks read per login — except with `mark=0`, which the web bell sends: it marks a row read when the row is opened, via `notifications/opened` with its `alert_id`, friction #23; `scope=group` (web and mobile) lists every location of a login that may switch locations, friction #24; read without `mark=0` it moves the read mark at every one of those locations — the bell's "Mark all read", re-audit F1-11). The list is the newest `models.NOTIFICATION_WINDOW` (40) rows. Each row: `id` (its alert_log id), `type`, `label`, `fired_at`, `review_id`, `module`, `priority`, `urgent`, `snippet` (the review's own words, ≤120 chars, for a review alert), `rating`, `resolved` (the thing the row is about is settled, where that is knowable — `client_api._notification_resolution`: a review alert whose review is answered, skipped or removed; a queued send, staff request or issue named by the row's `ref_kind`/`ref_id` no longer pending / no longer open; a `critical_low` with a newer one at the same location — and, for an urgent row with no knowable subject, once this login opened it), `resolves_on_open` (true on exactly those urgent rows: a client that opens one flips `resolved` itself), `opened` (this login opened it), `can_approve` + `draft` (a drafted, unflagged reply at the active location — the bell shows the text before its Post button), `delayed_action_id` + `can_undo` (a row about a queued send — `order_send_pending`, `schedule_publish_pending`; `can_undo` only while it is pending, at the active location, for a login `actions/<id>/cancel` would allow) and `request_id` + `request_kind` (`shift` | `time_off`) + `can_decide` (a staff request row; only while pending, at the active location, for a SCHEDULE_DRAFT holder — the `labor/shift-requests|time-off/<id>/decide` rule) — read from `alert_log.ref_kind`/`ref_id`, so the web bell offers Undo and Approve / Deny in place (web desk #4), `restaurant_id`, `location` (group scope only), `unread` (`models.notification_unread_rule`: after this login's read mark, never before the login existed, AND not opened — a row opened on either client is read on both). `GET notifications/unread-count` → `{count, urgent}` counted from that same logist (samened; `scope=group`, same window, same rules): `count` its `unread` rows, `urgent` its `urgent` and not `resolved` rows — so opening every row, or Mark all read, always clears it. The app icon's push badge (`push._badge_for`) is `models.unread_notification_count` — the same rule and window at the device's location), `POST notifications/opened` `{type, alert_id?, rec_key?}`, `GET notifications/engagement` (types sent a lot and never opened — a suggestion, not a change; `mine` is the same for this login's own phone — see Account, memory round), `POST account/send-test-push` (the caller's own devices only, delivered inline so the response is what APNs said). Team roles: `POST /api/account/team/invite` takes `role` (client | manager | member); `POST /api/account/team/<id>/role` `{role}` (owner-only) and its `/mobile/api` twin. Team access: `POST /api/account/team/<id>/access` and `/mobile/api/account/team/<id>/access` — `{permission, enabled}` and/or `{morning_brief}`, owner-only. Public: `GET/POST /i/<token>` (issue link; GET is side-effect free, POST `action=ack|resolve|ask_cover` — `ask_cover` with `name`, one of the coverage issue's suggested covers). Staff portal: `GET /staff/api/preshift`.

Memory round (9/29/26), same prefixes:
- **Goals are the target.** An account holder's goal is active at once and becomes the target every module judges that metric against (`owner_memory.target_for`: labels read "your goal of 26% by 12/31/26", a `target_source` / `food_cost_target_source` of `goal`, the DSR's budget line and prime-cost KPI target). A teammate's `POST goals` is PROPOSED and waits: the answer adds `proposed` (true → "Sent to the owner to confirm"). `GET goals` adds `proposed: [goal + proposed_by]` ("Your sales audit" for an audit's) — only goals on a metric this login may see — and `can_confirm` (an account holder). `POST goals/<id>/confirm` → `{ok, goal}` makes it the active goal on its metric; `POST goals/<id>/decline` → `{ok}`; both account-holder only (403) and 404 for a goal that is not a proposal of this restaurant. `GET goals` also adds `missed: [goal + label, unit, summary]` (re-audit R3): goals whose deadline passed without reaching them, retired from every prompt 14 days later, asked once — `POST goals/<id>/renew` `{days?: 30}` → `{ok, goal}` sets the same target with a new deadline, `POST goals/<id>/close` → `{ok}`; account-holder only (403), 404 unless it is a missed goal of this restaurant.
- **Undo asks why, once.** `POST actions/<id>/cancel` takes `{reason_code?, reason?}` (`delayed.UNDO_REASONS`: not_ready, wanted_changes, wrong_content, bad_timing, other; any other code is a 400). Undoing an automatic send (`delayed.TRUSTED_KINDS`: `schedule_publish`, `order_send`) counts against the trust that queued it: the answer adds `message` ("Undone — Cavnar AI will wait for a few clean runs before doing this on its own again") and, when no code came with it, `ask_why: {route: "/actions/<id>/why", options: [{code, label}]}`. `POST actions/<id>/why` `{reason_code, reason?}` → `{ok}`; 400 for a code not on the list, 404 when there is no undone action with that id.

### Outcomes and value — the rec-ROI contracts (`strategy_routes.py`, `client_api.py`, `mobile_api.py`; web and mobile twins share one body)
The outcome engine is `outcomes.py` / `metrics.py` / `value_delivered.py` (MODULE_OVERVIEW.md → Outcomes). Dates in machine fields are ISO; every owner-facing string (`label_text`, `reason`, `attribution_label`, `net_note`) is already M/D/YY.

- **`GET outcomes`** `?status=&ids=` → `{ok, outcomes: [row], caveat}` — newest 50, or with `ids` (comma-separated, ≤200) exactly those trackers of this restaurant however old (the timeline asks for the ones its page links to; a non-number is 400). Only rows this login may see (re-audit A26): the metric (food cost / waste need FOOD_COST_VIEW, `comp_rate` / `void_rate` need LOSS_VIEW) AND the recommendation behind it (`rec_learning.viewer_sees` on the linked episode: owner-only, a loss, a module it lacks) — an unseen row is simply absent. Each row also carries `checkin_key` — the recommendation key a check-in for it is sent against (`POST recs/checkin`), `null` when no recommendation stands behind the tracker (a manual one, a monthly reprice figure), so no surface offers a check-in the server would refuse. **Contract K7:** `summary` for a result the owner said they never made (check-in "no") carries no dollars — "…improved, but it isn't counted: you said the change wasn't made." — and `counts` (false for it) is what a surface colours by. Each row, besides the table's own columns and `metric_label`, `unit`, `summary`, `informational`:
  - `module` — who is credited: the recommendation's own module (`labor`, `inventory`, `reviews`, `marketing`, `intel`, `other`), never inferred from the metric when the recommendation named one.
  - `family` — the metric family (`labor_cost`, `food_cost`, `sales`, `guest_rating`, `reply_speed`, `comps`, `voids`).
  - `baseline_value`, `after_value`, `delta`, `delta_pct` — `delta` is after − baseline; `delta_pct` null when the baseline is 0 or unknown. `baseline_raw` is the before-window reading before any seasonal adjustment.
  - `baseline_kind` — `prior window` | `matched weekdays` | `same weeks last year`.
  - `attribution` — `none` | `associated` | `consistent` | `held` (null while tracking); `attribution_label` — the owner sentence; never claims cause (the `associated` level carries `caveat`'s sentence).
  - `concurrent` — `[{kind, label, date}]`, kind one of `tracker` | `accepted_rec` | `price_change` | `event` | `holiday` | `closure` | `sales_move` (a labor, food-cost, comp or void share whose sales moved past their band in the same weeks, "Sales per day rose 15% in the same weeks") | `trend` (the number was already moving this way before the change, by enough to explain the move — label "Already moving this way before the change") | `level_shift` (the number was already at the level it was read at, in the window that prompted the recommendation — label "Already at this level before the change started"). Any entry makes the result confounded: `counts` false, never a win or a loss in learning or delivered value (confidence re-audit B2 #5, #10), and `attribution_label` says it "isn't counted either way"; `concurrent_checked` false for a row evaluated before this existed (its grade is then at most `associated`). The grade is also capped at `associated` by the owner's check-in (conditions changed, or "no") — at evaluation, at the re-check and at the check-in alike.
  - Readings: a window counted in trading days (labor %, sales, one weekday's sales, comps, voids) is `unknown` unless ≥ 70% of its trading days were measured (`outcomes.MIN_COVERAGE`); comps and voids are read only over the days the POS was asked about them. Weekday-sensitive trackers (labor %, food cost %, sales, comps, voids, waste) run whole weeks: a `window_days` of 30 is measured as 28.
